Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành mẫu nến Doji trên khung đồ thị tuần sau hai tuần giảm điểm liên tiếp, 
cho thấy áp lực bán đã phần nào được kiểm soát. Trên khung ngày, nỗ lực hồi phục đầu 
tuần gặp lực cản tại đường SMA50 khiến VNINDEX quay đầu giảm điểm, tiếp tục chớm phá 
đáy ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên vùng hỗ trợ gần đã tạo hiệu ứng thu hút lực cầu bắt đáy, giúp chỉ 
số thu hẹp đà giảm trong phiên cuối tuần. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh khá tiêu cực từ vùng đỉnh 
đầu tháng 9, VNINDEX đang bước vào giai đoạn tìm kiếm điểm cân bằng trở lại và diễn biến 
giằng co điều chỉnh có thể còn kéo dài thêm trong các phiên kế tiếp. Tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn tính từ vùng đáy cuối tháng 7 vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại tại điểm đỡ gần quanh 1710 (đây cũng là chốt chặn cần 
giữ để bảo vệ được xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn). 
Kháng cự xa: 1820 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1775 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1710 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1670 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu 
Mã Giá đóng cửa tuần Biến động từ đầu tháng Giá khuyến nghị mua Giá mục tiêu Lợi nhuận  kỳ vọng Điểm nhấn đầu tư Báo cáo phân tích 
FRT 149.60 1.8% 143.40 165.00 15.1% 
- Sau khi phủ sóng các thành phố lớn, chuỗi Long Châu đang mở rộng sang các vùng cấp 2, 3. 
Năm 2026 dự kiến mở thêm 400-450 cửa hàng (+20%YoY) với DT trên mỗi cửa hàng cũ tăng 
trưởng 6%-8%/năm 
- Dư địa lấy thêm thị phần từ các cửa hàng nhỏ lẻ vẫn còn tương đối rộng, FRT đặt mục tiêu 
chiếm 50% thị phần doanh thu ngành bán lẻ dược phẩm, từ mức 17%-20% hiện tại 
- Tiềm năng dài hạn từ mảng tiêm chủng tương đối tích cực

## Document 314: European bank M&A is back with a bang

Source PDF: all report pdf/European bank M&A is back with a bang.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

European bank M&A has seen a resurgence in recent years, and
2027 should stay busy. Domestic deals still dominate, while
cross-border consolidation is harder but is also picking up.
Regulation is becoming more supportive, but politics and
execution still matter. Expect more deals, but a transformation
of Europe's banking sector is still some way oﬀ
Jesse Norcross
UniCredit and Commerzbank are at the centre of Europe’s closely watched
push towards cross-border banking consolidation

European bank M&A is back
European bank M&A has seen a strong resurgence, with several
large-scale deals making headlines. UniCredit’s high-stakes
takeover of Commerzbank, once deemed very unlikely but now
moving closer to completion, could be Europe’s most signiﬁcant
cross-border transaction in years. Italy remains one of Europe’s
bank M&A hotbeds, with multiple banks taking part in the ongoing
consolidation wave. Other banks have also been active, and we

expect M&A to remain elevated in 2027.

### Page 2

Year-to-date M&A deal value has already surpassed the 2025 total,
reaching €53bn including the UniCredit-Commerzbank transaction
and based on deals valued at €100m or more. Furthermore, deals
pending or intended total €39bn, which includes the potential
takeover of Banca Monte dei Paschi by Intesa Sanpaolo, currently
valued at around €34bn. All in, this would bring deal value back to
levels not seen since the early 2010s.
European bank M&A volumes have seen a

resurgence
(€bn)

Source: Reﬁnitiv, ING
The rationale for European bank M&A has changed. After the
ﬁnancial crisis, deals were mostly about repair, followed by a wave
of deals involving stronger domestic banks buying weaker rivals.
Today, the backdrop has shifted – proﬁtability and capital levels are
stronger, making the obvious cost-eﬃciency story less compelling

on its own. Instead, banks are using M&A to add scale, deposits and
fee-generating diversiﬁcation, while the need to close the gap with
US rivals remains a key driver.
The industrial logic has also strengthened, as ﬁxed costs are rising
in areas such as technology, AI, compliance, as well as personnel.
Rising investment requirements mean that remaining subscale is
becoming more expensive, even for highly proﬁtable banks.

### Page 3

Domestic dominates, but cross-border
competes
UniCredit-Commerzbank remains the sector’s most closely
watched deal and could become the ﬁrst major cross-border
acquisition since the ﬁnancial crisis. UniCredit has slowly been
building its stake rather than a clean takeover and is now deemed
to hold eﬀective control, despite currently owning around 50% of
the shares. Furthermore, it is also not entirely a leap into an
unfamiliar market, as UniCredit already has a signiﬁcant banking

business in Germany through its subsidiary, HVB.
The deal has also faced considerable pushback as well as political
and stakeholder opposition, but is now looking increasingly likely to
proceed. It has highlighted the diﬃculty of pulling oﬀ a large-scale
cross-border deal for European banks. Yet it is now becoming the
clearest live test of whether Europe is genuinely ready for cross-
border bank consolidation.
Domestic deals remain the rule, but we have seen other examples

of cross-border deals. These include BPCE’s acquisition of
Portuguese peer Novo Banco; Erste Bank's acquisition of Santander
Bank Polska; and Crédit Agricole acquiring IKB to increase its
presence in Germany. Some banks are also looking across the
pond, such as Santander, which acquired Webster Financial
Corporation in the US for USD12.5bn.
These transactions are important, but they do not yet amount to a
broad-based cross-border consolidation wave. Many involve banks

already familiar with the target market or assets that complement
an existing local business.
Selected transaction details

### Page 4

Source: Company reports, ING
Italy is still riding the wave
Italy has become one of Europe’s busiest M&A markets, riding a
new wave of consolidation, with Banca Monte dei Paschi (MPS) at
the epicentre. The bank completed the acquisition of Mediobanca in
2025, and this year approached Banco BPM and Banca Generali for
a merger as a counteroﬀensive to Intesa Sanpaolo’s hostile bid for
MPS.
The counteroﬀensive now appears less likely, as key shareholders

have opposed parts of MPS’s merger plans and have also backed
Intesa’s bid. The ECB has also provided authorisation to take control
of MPS, representing an important step for the €34bn takeover.
Alongside this, we had the BPER–Popolare di Sondrio merger earlier
in the year, adding to Italy’s consolidation.
Why is Italy so active in particular? Well, Italy remains quite
fragmented (see below) and still oﬀers a large domestic synergy
pool, even after the wave of M&A that has already taken place.

Furthermore, the country has meaningful industrial scale and oﬀers
further revenue diversiﬁcation opportunities for banks and insurers.
Although the obvious combinations are becoming less evident in
Italy, we expect this to continue to drive M&A activity in 2027.

country (2025)

It's not only about getting bigger
The deal mix is also broadening, as banks are increasingly focusing
on fee income as well as scale, while also stepping into capital-light
businesses. Deals in sectors such as wealth management, asset
management, payments & FX and insurance are likely to feature
prominently.
NatWest Group’s acquisition of Evelyn Partners is a good example.

The combination created the UK’s largest Private Banking and
Wealth Management business, while lifting Natwest’s fee income
by 20%. Similarly, BNP Paribas’s acquisition of AXA Investment
Managers in 2025 created a €1.5tn asset management business,
as the bank is seeking to compete with US asset management
rivals and grow its fee income. In Germany, Deutsche Bank and
DWS were reported to be exploring a potential minority investment
in the life insurance business Frankfurter Leben. This would mark

Deutsche’s return to life insurance after more than a decade.
Consolidation can support bank
performance
But does consolidation actually deliver better outcomes? The
evidence is not entirely clear-cut, but overall points in the right
direction. While increasing scale is not always the best use of
capital, in general, more consolidated markets tend to have more
stable and, in some cases, higher proﬁtability. The ECB is in favour
of further consolidation where appropriate, as it sees the beneﬁts of

diversiﬁcation and eﬃciency. On the other hand, capital can be
reduced following a merger. This could be a downside, as better
capitalised banks tend to enjoy lower funding costs and thus have
more room to lend to the real economy.
The outcome therefore depends on the trade-oﬀ between longer-
term synergies and the upfront capital, execution and integration
costs.
ECB data show an association between more concentrated banking
markets and proﬁtability. As the chart below shows, countries with

a high level of concentration (deﬁned by a higher Herﬁndahl-
Hirschman Index coeﬃcient) tend to have higher net interest
margins on average. Still, net interest margins in Europe are
structurally lower than in the US, where they tend to be between 3–
4%.

causal. Still, taken together, this suggests that consolidation can
improve banking markets.

Regulation is helping, but can get in the way
The Banking Union has made deals easier to assess: the ECB has
become the single prudential supervisor for large banks, capital
rules are more consistent, while resolution planning is now much

clearer than it was even a decade ago. But as the Draghi report
noted, the incomplete Banking Union means Europe’s banks remain
less proﬁtable than their US counterparts.
There is a shift underway, though. The EU Commission plans to
table a broad Banking Package in the ﬁrst quarter of 2027, with
measures covering cross-border capital and liquidity management,
deposit insurance, prudential rules and other items. For M&A, the
most important proposals include those to give banking groups

more ﬂexibility to allocate capital and liquidity more eﬃciently
within cross-border groups, while also applying more consistent

### Page 8

treatment to intra-group exposures. These remain proposals rather
than agreed implementations, meaning their ﬁnal scope and
timetable remain uncertain.
The AFME estimates that regulatory ring-fencing rules mean that
some €225bn of capital and €250bn of liquidity is trapped within
subsidiaries, which banks cannot freely move between parent and
subsidiaries. Freeing this up could also support more cross-border
transactions.

Source: ECB, ING
The European Commission is also bringing forward a new proposal
to replace the European Deposit Insurance Scheme (EDIS), which
could reduce national ring-fencing incentives and encourage more
cross-border M&A. CMDI reform has been passed this year, which
could make it easier to deal with failing banks, potentially making
consolidation easier as well, although the direct impact on M&A is

unclear.
The Danish compromise, which is now embedded into CRR3, allows
banks to risk-weight certain insurance subsidiaries rather than
deduct them from regulatory capital. This was clariﬁed by the EBA
in January 2026, and some banks such as UniCredit have received
authorisation to use the Danish Compromise methodology for its
insurance subsidiaries. This has improved the economics of

### Page 9

bancassurance and could support further bank-insurance
transactions.
On the other hand, national regulatory and political intervention
has also put the brakes on M&A in some cases, raising execution
costs and uncertainty. BBVA’s proposed acquisition of Sabadell in
2024 illustrates the problem. It took 539 days from the
announcement in May 2024 to the oﬀer failing in October 2025.
For us, the message seems clear that European regulators are
seeking to improve bank competitiveness, and they see

consolidation as an important part of this. Looking ahead,
regulation can improve the plumbing, but it is not a dealmaker.
Why 2027 should stay busy
The starting point for 2027 remains supportive, with bank
proﬁtability continuing to hold up well in 2026 while capital levels
remain solid. While the geopolitical, inﬂation and interest rate
backdrop poses headwinds, we still expect acquisition activity to
remain high. On the other hand, management teams will

increasingly need to choose between retaining greater capital
headroom in a less market-friendly environment, shareholder
payouts, or M&A.
The most likely deals are still domestic. Italy remains the M&A
market of choice, but other geographies could also stand out. The
Nordics are likely to see more partnerships and tech- and wealth-
focused deals. Spain and Portugal are already quite concentrated,
so we could see more bolt-ons. In the UK, challenger banks and

neobanks are gaining ground while deposit competition remains
intense, potentially leading to more activity. The Benelux has
potential catalysts following a series of government ownership
reductions and increasing inorganic ambitions.
Cross-border activity could also remain elevated, but we think the
real litmus test remains the UniCredit-Commerzbank deal, including
what happens after completion in terms of execution and
integration, a process that is likely to take a few years. The

regulatory environment should also become increasingly more

supportive for cross-border M&A from 2027 onwards.
Overall, 2027 could be another interesting year.
M&A is here to stay
European bank M&A has picked up in recent years, and we expect
more activity again in 2027, although we do not see a big bang. For
investors, positioning is inherently speculative. For bondholders, the
main way to gain is by owning the target entity's bonds. However,
this is not always the case, and bigger doesn’t always mean better,

as credit ratings don’t necessarily improve either following an
acquisition or merger.
We are not suggesting that a major change in the European
banking landscape is imminent, as many obstacles remain,
particularly with cross-border deals. But the direction of travel is
interesting: larger banks often outperform smaller banks, while
regulators continue to see beneﬁts from a deeper, more integrated
European banking union. Still, European banking markets remain

fragmented to diﬀering degrees, both between countries and
compared to the US, presenting both opportunities and obstacles.

### Page 1

Global air travel growth is set to rebound to 5% in 2027, but
airlines face tougher conditions. Demand remains resilient, but
the fuel price shock will spill over into 2027, while new aircraft
supply remains constrained. This creates divergent fortunes
across airlines, segments and regions
Rico Luman
The aviation industry now faces a growing challenge to deliver proﬁtable
growth as capacity remains constrained, costs rise, and geopolitical risks
intensify

The fuel-price shock will continue into 2027. Jet fuel
could account for close to 35% of airlines’ operating
costs in 2026, up from 27% in 2025. Tight reﬁnery
capacity and low inventories are likely to keep prices
elevated even if oil prices ease.
European air fares will have further to rise. Hedging
initially protected many European airlines, but that cover
will progressively expire during 2027. US fares have

Budget airlines are particularly exposed. Fuel represents

Premium carriers have more scope to raise fares without

Aircraft shortages will continue to constrain capacity.
Airbus and Boeing had a combined backlog of around

Asia is becoming the industry’s main long-term growth

most impactful

a result of the war, which has led to airspace closures and
passengers shifting to other destinations due to safety concerns.

growth with additional impacts elsewhere.

Jet fuel continues to trade high in Q4 2026
European jet fuel prices in $ per tonne (ARA)
Source: Reﬁnitiv, ING Research
Cost shock hit proﬁtability, but for some
airlines more than others

Jet fuel is often the largest or second-largest cost item for airlines.
In 2025, it accounted for 27% of total operational costs on average.
This share could move towards 35% in 2026 as a result of
persistently high jet fuel prices and elevated crack spreads, caused
by the loss of Middle Eastern supplies and tight global availability.
Further geopolitical developments remain highly uncertain. Even if
the Middle East conﬂict is resolved and oil prices retreat, tight

reﬁnery capacity and depleted inventories are likely to keep middle-
distillate prices, including jet fuel, elevated well into 2027.
High fuel prices impact proﬁts in US and
Europe, low cost carriers most vulnerable

### Page 7

Operational proﬁt (EBIT) in % per peiod
Source: Company reports, ING Research
Fuel hedging provides less cushioning in
2027; fares must respond
Airlines employ very diﬀerent strategies to protect themselves
against fuel price shocks, using derivatives based on crude oil or jet
fuel. European airlines generally hedged 40-90% of their jet fuel
consumption, cushioning the initial impact of the price shock.
However, the Middle East conﬂict has lasted too long to avoid a

signiﬁcant impact. Existing hedges gradually roll oﬀ, increasing
exposure to prevailing market prices. Ryanair, which remains (80%)
hedged until the second quarter of 2027, has already warned of
signiﬁcantly higher fares.
Outside, the picture is mixed, but hedge ratios are generally lower,
and airlines in the US and China, for instance, don’t hedge at all.
This means that two airlines operating similar routes can face very
diﬀerent fuel bills.
Airline tickets in the US responded much

faster to the cost shock than in Europe

### Page 8

Index ticket prices vs consumer price index (CPI, seasonally adjusted) in
the US and EU (Jan 2 = 100)
Source: St. Louis FED, Eurostat, ING Research
Fares already sharply higher in the US, less
so in Europe
The fuel price shock will increasingly feed through into higher fares.
US airlines’ practice of retaining relatively high exposure to fuel price
volatility resulted in an almost immediate pass-through, with ticket
prices rising solidly in the months after jet fuel prices started to rise.

It is also easier to raise prices when domestic competitors face a
similar cost shock.
The situation is diﬀerent in Europe, where hedging provided more
initial protection. This also means fares may still need to rise
signiﬁcantly if fuel prices fail to fall. Coverage gradually expires as
2027 progresses, leaving airlines increasingly exposed to higher
spot prices.
Airline proﬁts hit, but industry remains
proﬁtable
Global development of operational proﬁts (EBIT) per year

### Page 9

Source: IATA, *ING Research based on IATA
More factors are driving up costs,
particularly in Europe
Jet fuel is the clear outlier this year, but wage and maintenance
costs are also rising faster than the average price level. Borrowing
costs and aircraft leasing rates remain another factor, although the
impact depends on individual airlines’ strategies and ﬁnancial
structures.
European airlines also face rising costs from the European Union
Emissions Trading System (ETS) for ﬂights within the European

Economic Area. In addition, fuel suppliers are required to blend
sustainable aviation fuel (SAF) under ReFuelEU Aviation, starting at
2% and rising to 6% by 2030*. SAF still trades at a signiﬁcant
premium. This puts a price on emissions, and in combination, this
adds some $300 per tonne of jet fuel in 2026 (BNEF, ING Research).
Meanwhile, France and the Netherlands have either raised or plan
to raise ticket taxes, particularly on long-haul ﬂying, while Germany

has scaled its levy back and Sweden has abolished it.
High fuel prices put more pressure on low-
cost carriers
*A revision proposal is in progress for aviation, drawing a circle of 5,000 km around
Frankfurt for application of ETS, which would hit Middle East airlines most as they will
then be in scope. **In 2025, the blend rate stood at 2.8%

### Page 10

Low-cost carriers are particularly exposed because fuel accounts
for a larger share of their cost base, and proﬁt bases are more
vulnerable. High hedge ratios initially cushion volatility, but these
hedges gradually roll oﬀ. The more challenging environment is
already visible in the performance of several European low-cost
carriers and will remain an important theme in 2027 if fuel prices
stay elevated.
Premium carriers have more room to manoeuvre. Demand for

premium seats (business, premium economy) is less price-
sensitive, providing airlines with greater scope to pass on higher
costs. Both United Airlines and Delta Airlines reported little impact
after price increases of 15-20% in the second to third quarters.
Passengers are booking later, but airlines continue to indicate a
strong propensity to ﬂy, especially in premium and premium-
economy classes. A minority also accounts for a large share of
ﬂights. In the Netherlands, for example, 25% of people account for

75% of journeys. These frequent travellers tend to be less price-
sensitive.
There has therefore been some demand destruction wiping out part
of the underlying growth. So far, though, the propensity to travel
has remained resilient.
Aviation remains proﬁtable, but margins hit
a post-pandemic low
The fuel shock has signiﬁcantly aﬀected margins. On average,
proﬁts in the global industry are expected to drop meaningfully
below 5%. Airlines including Ryanair, Southwest Airlines and AirAsia

have scaled back capacity on unproﬁtable routes as elevated fuel
prices persisted longer than expected.
According to global aviation consultancy Cirium, around 1% of
global air travel capacity will be removed heading into the Northern
Hemisphere winter of 2026-27. In less mature markets, maintaining
volumes while passing on the higher fuel bill is more diﬃcult.
Airlines may also prioritise less fuel-eﬃcient, older-generation
aircraft over capacity growth.

### Page 11

Industry headwinds could become a catalyst for further
consolidation, ranging from minority stakes to full takeovers.
Fares could retreat if fuel prices ease, but
don't expect too much
Dynamic and algorithm-based pricing is becoming increasingly
common. This helps airlines optimise already historically high load
factors (84% in 2026 so far) but can also contribute to greater fare
volatility, especially as passengers book later amid uncertainty.
If fuel prices ease in 2027, fares could drop back. Yet relatively

strong demand, constrained aircraft availability and general
inﬂation mean a return to early-2026 fare levels looks unlikely.
Cargo beneﬁts from high-tech demand and
supply-chain volatility
Air cargo continues to perform well as a fast transport option in an
increasingly volatile and less predictable world. Volume growth
remained almost 5% year-to-date, while freight rates remained
attractive for carriers.
While e-commerce drove much of the growth in recent years,

digitalisation and large-scale AI investment increasingly support
shipments of high-value technology products. Strong cargo
performance helped cushion pressure on airline results in 2026.
European carriers also beneﬁted from reduced available freight
capacity from Middle Eastern competitors.
Freighter capacity received a boost during the pandemic through
passenger-to-freighter conversions, but that boost has largely run
its course. New-generation A350 and Boeing 777 freighters should

add capacity in the coming years.
Aircargo volume resilient in disruptive
geopolitical environment

### Page 12

Source: IATA, ING Research
Future demand 2027 and onwards
Aviation’s centre of gravity is increasingly
shifting eastwards
The centre of gravity of global aviation is increasingly shifting
eastwards. Trips per capita are expected to see strong increases
over the next two decades, particularly in Asia. This matters even
more because these markets are far more populous.
Until recently, around 90% of the world’s population did not ﬂy in a
given year, but this is gradually changing. As a result, ﬂeet growth is

expected to be strongest in Asia, with its share of the global aircraft
ﬂeet increasing from around 33% to approximately 45%.
People still expected to ﬂy more, especially
in India
Trips per capita per year in various countries

### Page 13

Source: S&P, Airbus, ING Research
India is becoming a powerhouse for future
airline growth
Air travel is strongly correlated with GDP and household income. As
Asia’s middle class expands, the potential customer base for air
travel swells. For future aviation demand, this income eﬀect is even
more important than population growth alone.
Younger generations also appear keen to travel, adding to the
potential in countries such as India and Indonesia. Increasingly

internationally dispersed families provide another structural source
of demand.
India stands out here. Strong economic growth, rising household
incomes and a huge population oﬀer strong potential. Airlines are
positioning themselves accordingly, illustrated by historically large
aircraft orders from IndiGo and Air India. India’s aircraft ﬂeet is
expected to grow by around 8% annually on average over the next
two decades.
China and India are the growth engines of

global aviation in the decades ahead

### Page 14

Source: S&P Global, Airbus, ING Research
Supply-side developments
Aircraft scarcity keeps capacity tight and
lease rates elevated
Supply constraints have been curbing aviation growth since the
post-pandemic recovery and remain a major factor. The share of
stored aircraft dropped below 10% by the end of 2025, its lowest
level since 2019. Even several A380s (the largest aircraft) have
returned to service.
More than half of the global commercial aircraft ﬂeet is leased.

Tight capacity is consequently reﬂected in relatively high used-
aircraft values and lease rates.
Airbus and Boeing had a combined record backlog of around
15,000 aircraft by mid-2026. Production and deliveries continue to
lag order intake after seven consecutive years of subdued deliveries
from 2019 onwards. Demand for new-generation aircraft remains
strong, as illustrated by major recent orders from Avolon and
Turkish Airlines.
Production could ﬁnally return to pre-pandemic levels in 2027.

Chinese manufacturer COMAC, meanwhile, is making progress in
China in Asia, but Western certiﬁcation remains distant.

### Page 15

New airplane deliveries continue on a
recovery track now with Boeing rebounding
Number of delivered commercial airplanes by manufacturers Airbus &
Boeing per year
Source: Company reports *Company forecast
Why aircraft supply remains constrained
Production has still not caught up with pre-pandemic levels
and is unlikely to do so in the next ﬁve years. Aircraft
manufacturing is recovering, but delays and large backlogs
push delivery dates into the 2030s. Airlines are therefore forced

to keep older aircraft ﬂying for longer.
Suppliers are still struggling to ramp up. Six years after the
sharp pandemic downturn, aviation supply chains have still
not fully normalised. Stringent safety and certiﬁcation
requirements mean manufacturers rely on a limited number of
specialised suppliers. Engines and sophisticated cabin
components still have long lead times, while skilled workers
remain scarce.
Engine issues continue to aﬀect available capacity. The peak

impact of the Pratt & Whitney inspection programme after an
identiﬁed production issue has passed, but checks and
maintenance continue to constrain parts of the A320neo-
family ﬂeet and absorb scarce maintenance capacity.

Aircraft production still has a long way to go

Orderbook Airbus & Boeing: number of commercial airplanes
Source: Company reports
Delivery catch-up is crucial for both
capacity and efﬁciency
New aircraft are critical not only to accommodate growth, but also
to improve eﬃciency. The average age of the global ﬂeet has
increased from around 13 to 15 years since 2019 due to production
constraints.
This matters increasingly as older units require more maintenance

at a time when skilled personnel and maintenance capacity are
scarce; they also consume signiﬁcantly more fuel and therefore
emit more.
Boeing is ﬁnally regaining altitude
supporting deliveries
Boeing is ﬁnally making a sustained comeback following an
unprecedented period of disruption stemming from the 737 MAX
accidents, the pandemic and subsequent production and quality
problems. It received certiﬁcation for its 737 MAX 7 and also the
737 MAX 10 recently. Boeing plans to increase production for the

family from 38 to 47 a month.

### Page 17

Higher 737 production should support deliveries in 2027 and
gradually bring pre-pandemic production levels back into sight. This
is hugely important for Boeing-dependent airlines such as Ryanair
and Southwest. Progress on certiﬁcation of the 777-9 is also
important for long-haul ﬂeet renewal.
Fleet renewal is currently the most
important decarbonisation lever
Air travel continues to grow, and associated emissions tend to
continue to rise further as well. Sustainable aviation fuels are

labelled as an important industry pathway to decarbonise, but
globally, blend rates still hover below 1% in 2026.
The closure of airspaces, too, has created ineﬃciencies and extra
emissions over the last few years, undermining gains elsewhere.
Around 63% of the global ﬂeet still consists of previous-generation
aircraft. Replacing them can reduce fuel consumption by 10-25% or
more, making ﬂeet renewal one of the most eﬀective near-term
decarbonisation levers available to airlines.

The challenge is to deliver proﬁtable growth
in a more disruptive world
For airlines, the challenge heading into 2027 is therefore not simply

ﬁnding suﬃcient demand. Relatively poor punctuality, with only
around 61% of ﬂights departing on time in 2026 year-to-date, is a
signal.
The globally operating industry faces more turbulence and
uncertainty amid intensiﬁed geopolitical risks. The challenge is to
deliver proﬁtable growth in a world of persistently constrained
capacity, higher costs and more frequent disruption.

Our ﬁrst impression on
Anthropic’s income statement
in IPO prospectus
從  Anthropic IPO 招股說明書財務報表，我們看到了公司 10 倍速的
增⻑，超分散的客戶結構，近 50GW 算⼒合約及兩項驚⼈的⾮現⾦費
⽤
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS
OCT 27 ∙ PAID

READ IN APP
Executive summary
Anthropic 跟  OpenAI 兩⼤⼤語⾔模型⿓頭應該是今年及明年美國 IPO 的重頭
戲，也是⽬前全球 AI 數據算⼒中⼼基礎建設及產業鏈最主要的兩⼤客戶，這兩
家公司的營收增⻑，獲利變化，算⼒租⽤契約都跟產業鏈息息相關，⽬前看來
Anthropic 3Q26 營收季增確實有趨緩現象，但 3Q26 及 2026 營收年增依然有
10 倍以上增速，值得關注的是不同於 AI 數據算⼒中⼼如 AWS, ⾕歌  Cloud, 微
軟  Intelligent Cloud 及新興雲  SpaceXSI, Oracle Cloud, CoreWeave,
Nebius 僅靠著少數⼤語⾔如 Anthropic, OpenAI, Meta, 微軟及⾕歌等⼤語
⾔模型公司的基礎建設積壓訂單，看起來產業鏈⾵險極⾼，但⿓頭  Anthropic
及其他同業卻有著⾮常健康分散的客戶結構。⾄於未來 10 年近 50GW 算⼒合約
（ AI 數據算⼒中⼼的積壓訂單）是否能執⾏？及如果把⾮現⾦項⽬如可轉換票

據，衍⽣性負債算⼊ GAAP 費⽤， Anthropic 的短期季度虧損似乎將隨著負債
及估值擴⼤⽽持續放⼤，那何時將結束呢？

10x yoy growth in sales

Anthropic 將於  2027 年採購近 200 億美元  AMD MI455 Helios AI 算⼒
機櫃；
Anthropic 將於  Aug 2026-2033 租⽤ Nscale Global 近 446 億美元算
⼒（ 7 5/12 年⻑約，年均 60 億美元算⼒合約）；
Anthropic 將於  2026-2033 租⽤ Lambda 近 413 億美元算⼒（ 7.5 年⻑

約，年均 55 億美元算⼒合約）。
算⼒是 Anthropic 最主要的營運成本，⽽算⼒效率則是決定公司營運效率與營
收的關鍵要素。由研究驅動的演算法改進（涵蓋訓練與推論優化），結合多晶
⽚算⼒架構，在營收規模擴張的同時，⼤幅提升了算⼒效率。這使得公司在每
投⼊⼀美元算⼒投資時，所能產⽣的模型智能⽔準以及創造的業務營收均能持
續提升。這是為何我們預估 Anthropic 隨著營收在 2H26-2030 的持續增⻑，
Anthropic 的營業利潤率及將股權激勵⾮現⾦費⽤加回的調整後營業利潤率應
該有持續改善的空間。
訓練效率（ Training Eﬃciency ）
Anthropic 的研究⼈員持續研發出具乘數效應的算⼒優化技術。更重要的是，
Claude 本⾝已越來越深⼊協助研發與⼯程⼯作，以打造後續各代模型版本；
公司稱這種遞迴動態為閉環（ closing the loop ），由新⼀代模型加速下⼀代
模型的研發，使這些效益在模型世代交替間產⽣複合式增⻑。
推論效率（ Inference Eﬃciency ）

研究團隊在推論端實現了顯著的算⼒乘數效益 —— ⼤幅降低在提供特定模型效
能表現時所需的算⼒；同時，⼯程團隊也⼤幅優化了⽣產環境中算⼒資源的利
⽤率。這使得公司能為客戶提供更⾼的價值，並隨著時間推移從現有算⼒基礎
上創造更⾼的營收。
多晶⽚算⼒架構（ Multi-Chip Compute Approach ）
Anthropic 透過專屬的編排層（ orchestration layer ），使  Claude 能原⽣在
四⼤主流晶⽚系列上進⾏訓練與推論服務： AWS Trainium 、 Google TPU 、
NVIDIA GPU 以及  AMD GPU ，這讓公司能針對價格與效能最佳化各項⼯作負
載。這項具備⾼度彈性的架構歷時多年與巨額投資打造。它不僅擴⼤了可獲得
的算⼒供應、降低對單⼀供應商的依賴，更能讓公司持續將不同⼯作負載指派
⾄最適⽤的硬體上，將適配新晶⽚平台所需的時間⼤幅縮短。

### Page 5

Source: Anthropic
Source: Anthropic, Andrew Lu on global semis/techs
Personnel expenses
⼈事費⽤主要是由近  7 成的股權激勵費⽤（ Stock-based Compensation ）
所組成，外加現⾦薪酬，及其他福利與税費，這其中包含三⼤  non-GAAP 的
股票選擇權（ Stock Options ）、限制性股票單位（ Restricted stock unit,
RSUs ）及限制性股票獎勵（ Restricted stock awards, RSAs ）。  我們認為
這次  IPO 上市可能將帶來的⼀次性  SBC 費⽤激增，尤其是部分原先附帶上市 /
績效條件（ Performance Condition ）的特定  RSUs 將直接達成歸屬條件，
因此在該招股說明書⽣效時，將認列⼀筆額外且顯著的股權激勵費⽤，但因為

### Page 6

這並⾮現⾦項⽬，所以在調整後並不會影響整體獲利及營運現⾦流，但差距影
響近 11 個百分點的獲利率。
Source: Anthropic
Change in fair value of convertible notes,
derivative liabilities, and SAFEs
這些⾮經常性費⽤的本質是可轉債與衍⽣⼯具內含「看漲期權（ Call Option ）
的費⽤。
投資⼈持有的權利：當  Amazon 、 Google 等機構購買  Anthropic 的可轉換
票據（ Convertible Notes ）或簽訂未來認購協議（衍⽣性負債）時，他們不
只擁有⼀筆借給公司的債券本⾦，更同時擁有在未來以約定價格（通常附帶折
扣或估值上限  Cap ）將本⾦轉換成公司股票的權利。
期權價值隨估值暴增：當  Anthropic 的企業估值從幾百億美元⼀路飆升⾄幾
千億甚⾄上兆美元時，投資⼈⼿中這張「低價換股憑證」的潛在增值利益極其
龐⼤。對投資⼈（資產⽅）⽽⾔，這筆投資的公允價值暴增；但對發⾏⽅

Anthropic （負債⽅）⽽⾔，這代表公司未來必須交付價值遠⾼於當初借款⾦
額的股權。
可以從下⽅圖表看出來， Anthropic GAAP 財報的主要虧損就是因為這可轉換
票據及衍⽣性負債因公司估值及股價增加⽽擴⼤虧損，但因為這並⾮現⾦項
⽬，所以在調整後並不會影響整體獲利及營運現⾦流。⼀旦公司完成  IPO ，這
些可轉債與衍⽣協議將全數債轉股（轉換為特別股或普通股），屆時這筆⾦融
負債會直接轉移⾄股東權益（ Equity ）項下，未來便不再需要每季按公允價
值重估，也不會再持續侵蝕損益表。
⼀、會計處理選擇：採⽤「公允價值選擇權（ Fair Value Option ）」
會計準則依據：依據美國會計準則  ASC 825 （⾦融⼯具），公司選擇按「公允
價值（ Fair Value ）」計量發⾏的可轉換票據（ Convertible Notes ）。

### Page 7

資產負債表與損益表認列：可轉債以估計的公允價值列於資產負債表。⽽每季
度公允價值的變動，直接認列於合併損益表中的可轉換本票、衍⽣性負債及
SAFE 公允價值變動」科⽬。
選擇動機：消除傳統拆分模式（ Bifurcation Model ）下將「負債」與「權益」
分別獨⽴核算的複雜性。
⼆、 Amazon 與  Google 協議所衍⽣之「衍⽣性負債（ Derivative
Liabilities ）」
針對  2023 年與  2024 年與  Amazon 及  Google 簽訂的本票購買協議
（ Note Purchase Agreements ），公司認列了代表「依約有義務在未來發⾏
新可轉債」的衍⽣性負債。這些衍⽣性負債同樣以預估公允價值計量，每期的
估值波動亦全數計⼊同⼀損益表科⽬。
Source: Anthropic, Convertible notes outstanding and purchase
agreements
三、估值的⾼度主觀性與關鍵假設變數
該估值極度依賴複雜且主觀的假設，任何變更都會對損益表認列⾦額產⽣重⼤

衝擊（ Material Impact ）：
結算情境發⽣的機率與時間點：例如合格股權融資、 IPO 、直接上市（ Direct
Listing ）或併購 / 控制權變更的時程。
底層標的股權的公允價值：即公司整體估值的暴漲或暴跌。
動態轉換價格與折價幅度：依不同輪次票據條款、發⾏經過時間、估值上限
（ Cap ）與折扣率（ Discount ）計算。

### Page 8

持股上限與展期機制（ Ownership Cap & Rollover Mechanism ）：
條款規定換股⽐例設有上限，超出上限的未轉換本⾦將重新發⾏成「不計息的
展期票據（ Rollover Note ）」，且該展期票據仍需持續進⾏公允價值衡量。由
於未來融資規模與結構具⾼度不確定性，需深度主觀判斷有多少⽐例會「轉成
股權」或「轉為展期票據」。
四、 SAFE （未來股權簡單協議）的處理現況
2023 年發⾏的  SAFE 同樣採⽤上述情境公允價值模型計量。
現狀清空：所有  SAFE 已於  2023 與  2024 年度全數轉換為可贖回可轉換特
別股。
截⾄  2024 年底、 2025 年底及  2026 年上半年（截⾄  6 ⽉  30 ⽇），帳上已
無任何未結清的  SAFE 。未來持續⾯臨公允價值波動與估值⾵險的核⼼部位，
已全部聚焦在上述可轉換本票（ Convertible Notes ）及其展期票據
（ Rollover Notes ）。
Source: Anthropic

## Document 382: Q3 China GDP above expectations, but downside risks remain (2026-11-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Q3 China GDP above expectations, but downside risks remain (2026-11-01).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#china #asia #aud
Q3 China GDP above expectations, but downside risks remain
By Elliot Clarke
Head of International Economics, Westpac
16:03 October 19 2026 Share
Stimulus is required if success in 2026 is to be sustained through 2027 and 2028.
China GDP surprised to the upside in Q3 2026, quarterly growth accelerating from 0.9% in Q2 to 1.2%
in Q3. Annual growth lifted from 4.3% to 4.5% as a result; though, on a year-to-date basis, growth
edged down 0.1ppts to 4.6%. The Q3 result keeps authorities on track to achieve their 4.5-5.0%ytd

ambition for 2026, and also provides a little more time to accelerate domestic demand into 2027.

### Page 2

Broad-based stimulus is required because net exports’ contribution to growth will likely abate in coming
quarters now that the trade surplus is back at record highs, and as many Chinese firms are
increasingly splitting their focus between expanding domestic capacity and offshore production
networks.

While annual retail sales growth accelerated from 0.4% to 1.1% in September, the Q3 year-to-date
growth pulse of 1.1% is just a quarter of the gain reported a year ago. Automobiles, construction
materials and jewellery have been particular heavy detractors over the past year, but most of the other
sub-categories also lack momentum. As we detailed in our September Market Outlook, this is not
because households are cash constrained, but rather as sentiment is extremely weak and the outlook
for employment and wealth highly uncertain. Key for wealth is that house prices for new and
established homes continue to decline month-on-month (respectively 0.1% and 0.3% in September)
five years into the downturn. Property investment’s ongoing decline (-20.6%ytd) suggests this
persistent weakness in sales and prices is demand driven.

While total fixed asset investment has been an outright negative for annual GDP growth since April, a
recovery is not beyond either the public or private sector’s capacity. Rather, across the economy,
entities are waiting for direction and incentive from the Central Government. This is likely to come by
early-2027 to offset the loss of support from trade, and as downside risks to global growth become
more apparent. Helpfully, industrial production continues to grow at a robust pace, 5.3%ytd, borrowing
rates for businesses and households are low, and authorities are beginning to provide banks with
additional lending capacity. For larger businesses, anecdotes suggest officials are also encouraging
major institutional investors to increase their structural allocation to domestic equities, building lasting 
appetite for new equity issuance, which in turn will fund future waves of real economic capacity and
income generation.

We remain comfortable with the view that Chinese GDP growth will hold at or slightly above 4.5%
through 2026–2028. But, the longer the Central Government waits to trigger new real investment
across both the private and public sectors, the higher the likelihood of poor sentiment becoming
entrenched and downside risks crystallising. If authorities wait until after net exports’ contribution has
faded, even holding annual aggregate growth above 4.0% could become a significant challenge. For
this reason, we expect material stimulus initiatives to be announced in the next 3 to 6 months to reset
the property sector and turn the business investment trend.

momentum meaningfully above 
the RBA’s current expectations.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
August inflation came in soft...
Inflation moderated in August following July’s strong 
outcome. The CPI rose 0.4% in the month, in line with our 
expectation but slightly below market consensus of 0.5%, 
taking the annual rate to 4.0%. On a seasonally adjusted 
basis, the CPI increased 0.7%mth, underscoring the impact of 
seasonal discounting in August and the start of the financial 
year repricing in July.
More closely watched are the measures of underlying inflation 
including the Trimmed Mean (TM) and market services. 
The TM rose 0.2% in August, in line with our expectations 
but again softer than the market, leaving the annual pace 
unchanged at 3.6% for a third consecutive month. Meanwhile, 
market services excluding volatiles & holiday travel increased 
0.2%mth/4.1%yr. Notable strength (three-month annualised, 
seasonally adjusted) was seen in sports participation (10.9%), 
clothing repair (7.0%), restaurant meals (6.8%) and other 
household services (5.2%). For full analysis of the August CPI, 
see “August CPI – Came in on our expectations ”.
The expenditure-weighted share of the CPI basket 
experiencing positive price shocks eased to 11% in August 
from 15% in July, while the share experiencing negative price 
shocks declined from 17% to just below 13%. The majority of 
the positive shocks were concentrated in consumer durables 
On a three-month moving average basis, the net momentum 
share (positive minus negative) rose to –4.5% from –7.7% 
in July and a trough of –15.8% in May, suggesting the 
disinflationary impulse may be nearing exhaustion. 
... with September due for a moderate pick-up
Looking ahead to September, early indicators painted a 
mixed picture. The NAB business survey showed retail price 
growth slowing to 0.9%qtr from 1.1%qtr in August, with 
labour cost growth easing to 1.7%qtr from 1.8%qtr, while 
purchase cost growth edged up to 2.4%qtr from 2.3%qtr. 
Nevertheless, all measures remain well above their long-run 
averages while the gap between purchase costs and final 
prices continues to widen, indicating businesses are facing 
margin compression (see here for further detail). Meanwhile, 
consumers’ year-ahead inflation expectations held steady 
at 4.9% in September (easing slightly at 2dp) but surged to 
5.3% in October likely reflecting higher fuel prices, with retail 
petrol and diesel averaging $2.20/l (+8.2%mth) and $2.68/l 
(+7.7%mth). We do believe the auto fuel prices are likely to see 
a near term peak in October.  Meanwhile, global shipping costs 
have also surged recently amid renewed Middle East tensions.
Sep quarter unrevised but Dec upgraded significantly
Justin Smirk, Senior Economist
Neha Sharma, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The September CPI is expected to rise 0.6% (0.62% to 2dp)
with the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr. Our seasonally adjusted
estimate increases 0.5% (0.48% to 2dp). For the monthly
TM, we expect a 0.3% (0.26% to 2dp) lift, holding the annual

pace steady at 3.6%. On a 3m/3m basis, the TM is tracking
a 1.0% gain in September (noting that while this measure is
conceptually different to the quarterly TM it still serves as a
useful guide). For market services excluding volatiles & holiday
travel we have pencilled in another 0.2% rise, slowing the
annual pace slightly to 4.0% (a nine-month low).
The largest contributors are recreation & culture (+0.27ppt),
driven wholly by holiday travel & accommodation. Transport

(+0.23ppt) makes a solid contribution, with gains in fuel
(+0.26ppt) partly offset by motor vehicles (–0.02ppt). Housing
also contributes (+0.13ppt), with rents and new dwellings
each adding 0.02ppt while property rates add 0.08ppt.
The key detractors in the month are food & non-alcoholic
beverages (–0.08ppt) and garments (–0.04ppt).
Key details for the month of September
Food & non-alcoholic beverages are expected to fall
0.5%mth, easing the annual pace to 2.8%yr. The weakness is

concentrated in a 3.7%mth decline in fruit & vegetables, while
non-alcoholic beverages are also expected to fall 1.5%mth.
Meals out & takeaway is forecast to rise 0.1%mth, easing the
annual rate to 3.9%.
Housing is expected to rise 0.6% in September, slowing the
annual pace to 5.5%yr. Component-wise:
• Rents are expected to rise 0.3%mth and 3.7%yr. CPI rent
inflation is nearing its peak, consistent with the moderation
in asking rent growth. We expect rent inflation to ease to

3.6%yr by end-2026 and 2.5%yr by end-2027.
• New dwelling prices, which capture only the building
component of construction costs, are expected to rise
0.2%mth and 4.9%yr. Ongoing weakness in established
housing markets would be increasing builders’ difficulty in
passing through higher costs to new builds. As a result, we
expect new dwelling inflation to moderate to 4.5%yr by
end-2026 and 2.1%yr by end-2027.
Transport inflation is expected to rise 2.0%mth in September,

lifting the annual pace to 7.7%yr reflecting an 8.3%mth
increase in fuel prices following higher oil prices through the
month. Motor vehicle prices and spare parts are expected to
fall 0.7%mth and 1.3%mth, respectively. In NSW, a one-year
vehicle registration discount between $80-100 came into
effect from 1 September, which is likely to weigh on the other
services in respect of motor vehicles component.
Recreation & culture is expected to rise 2.1%mth driven by a

4.4%mth increase in holiday travel, partly offset by a 0.4%
decline in audio, visual & computing equipment & services and
a 1.3% fall in sporting services.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Risks to September baseline
We view the risks around the September CPI outcome as broadly 
balanced. Waning spending momentum outside of fuel may make 
it difficult for businesses to pass through higher costs in the near 
term. However, some of these pricing pressures may be deferred 
rather than forgone. The October ban on card surcharging may 
prompt a greater-than-usual degree of price resetting, allowing 
businesses to recover some of the margin pressures that have built 
up in recent months.
Risks to housing are mixed. Trade materials suppliers continued 
to announce above-average price increases through September 
(around 8%). While soft conditions in the established housing 
market may limit the extent to which these costs are passed 
through to new dwelling prices, they nevertheless present an 
upside risk to housing-related inflation, particularly maintenance 
& repair and insurance costs. Growth in advertised rents also 
continues to ease but these new leases take some time to flow 
through to average rents paid measured in the CPI. 
A kick up in October ...
We have a solid preliminary estimate for the October CPI of 
0.6%mth lifting the annual pace to 4.8%yr. It is true that the key 
driver is an 8.9% increase in auto fuel but we estimate the TM to 
lift 0.4%mth/3.7%yr highlighting broader inflationary pressures. 
We expect that the removal of credit charge surcharges and 
resulting increase in retail prices, at most, will contribute 0.1ppt 
but only just so see it as more of a rounding error. Nevertheless, 
these changes come at a time of a significant upstream price 
pressure, particularly for SMEs, so there is some risk of broader 
price adjustments. As such, we expect marginally larger than 
usual price adjustments in October particularly in hospitality 
and household services. Being the first month of the quarter the 
October lift sets a higher base for the quarterly average estimates 
resulting in an upgrade to our December quarter estimate.
We have included a table with a monthly profile for the CPI, 
seasonally adjusted CPI and the TM out to June 2027. At 
June 2027 we have an annual pace of 3.7%yr for the CPI and 
seasonally adjusted CPI and 3.3%yr for the TM. There is also a 
table breaking down the monthly components of the CPI through 
the December quarter. 
... highlights stronger inflationary pressure ...
Our September quarter CPI estimate is unrevised at 1.3%qtr 
with the annual pace steady at 3.9%yr. At the margin there 
was a 0.04ppt increase due to a lift in auto fuel but this is 
1.29%qtr at two decimal places so still rounds to 1.3%. 
The seasonally adjusted CPI gains 1.1% in the September 
quarter highlighting that part of the increase is due to usual 
seasonality. Our TM estimate for Q3 has a small downward 
revision of 0.03ppt to 0.88% at two decimal place so our 
published estimate is also unrevised at 0.9%qtr. 
... resulting in an upward revision to Q4 2026
Westpac’s December quarter CPI estimate has been boosted 
from a 0.5%qtr to 1.3%qtr. Driving most of this change from 
our previous estimate is the 15.3% increase in autofuel, it

was 1.9%, contributing 0.55ppt to the 0.8ppt increase in the 
quarterly estimate. Outside of a 0.09ppt contribution to the 
revision from holiday travel & accommodation (revised up from 
4.2% to 5.6%) all else was via small contributions for a large

increase in our September quarter TM estimate from 0.7%qtr 
to 0.9%qtr.
A disinflationary impulse kicks in for 2027
We now expect auto fuel prices to peak in October (240 cpl 
for petrol, 284 cpl for diesel) and then to trend lower through 
2027 (184 cpl for petrol and 194 cpl for diesel by December 
2027) leading to a 13.6% falling in auto fuel in the first half 
of 2027 subtracting 0.46ppt off the CPI over the same period 
of time. For a detailed description on how we constructed the 
auto fuels profile please see: “ Persistent energy pressures 
have further to run ” 
The first half of 2027 will also see an almost 5% decline in 
holiday travel & accommodation which will subtract a further 
0.29ppt off the CPI. However, outside of that any broader 
moderation in inflation is expected to be more muted hence 
we have the TM printing 0.7%qtr for each quarter in 2027.  
Critical to this moderation in core inflation is our expect step 
down in dwelling price inflation on the back of falling estab -
lished home prices. From a 1.1%qtr increase in Q3 2026 we 
see dwelling price increasing by 0.7% in the Q4 2026, and 
then 0.6% in both Q1 and Q2 2027 before dropping to 0.5%qtr 
in Q3 and Q4 2027. This will see dwelling price inflation in the 
CPI drop from 5.4%yr at June 2026 to 3.0%yr by June 2027 
then down to 2.2%yr by December 2027. 
At least for the near term a key uncertainty remains the extent 
to which businesses are able to pass through ongoing labour 
and non-labour cost pressures and how this unwinds through 
2027 as energy prices ease and the domestic economy slows 
under the weight of higher interest rates and falling estab -
lished home prices. There is a risk that firms face greater 
resistance to price increases if discretionary household spend -
ing softens by more than expected. That said, these headwinds 
may be at least partially offset by the stronger starting point 
for household incomes, which has proven more resilient than 
previously anticipated, and by higher energy prices due to the 
ongoing conflict in the Middle East.
That said, there is also the risk that the disinflationary pulse by 
the second half of 2027 could be greater than what we antici-

and thus a more significant moderation in price setting behav -

than we anticipate. For while we have spot crude prices to fall 
to about pre-crisis levels by end 2027, a combination of a slow 
recovery in refining capacity, constrained return of physical 
supplies and higher shipping cost keep Australian fuel prices 
above their pre-conflict level. Should all these factors improve 
fast than we expect, and auto fuel prices return to pre-conflict 
level if not lower, then headline inflation could dip below 2%yr 
and the TM would be closer to 2.5%yr (the mid point if the 
RBA’s inflation target), by end 2027.

## Document 454: “价格不敏感”的买家碰上了“价格不敏感”的卖家！高盛解读：黄金为何跌破4000，为何后续更可能下跌

Source PDF: all report pdf/“价格不敏感”的买家碰上了“价格不敏感”的卖家！高盛解读：黄金为何跌破4000，为何后续更可能下跌.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⻩⾦正在经历⼀场教科书式的失灵 —— 实际利率下⾏、股市承压、地缘危机三重利好同时
在场，⾦价却跌破 4000 美元，较历史⾼点累计回落 30% 。⾼盛揭⽰，幕后推⼿并⾮对冲基
⾦，⽽是主权实体为应对流动性需求被迫抛售⻩⾦储备，五周内 195 亿美元砸出，速度远超
央⾏每⽉ 85 吨的接盘节奏。头⼨尚未出清， 3850 美元或将成为下⼀道防线。
摘要：
⻩⾦正在经历⼀场教科书式的失灵。所有应当推动⾦价上涨的宏观因素同时出现，⾦价却反向
下跌，并于上周五跌破 4000 美元关键整数关⼝。⾼盛的最新分析揭⽰了这场异常抛售背后的真
实推⼿，并警告市场结构尚未出清，下⾏⻛险仍未消除。
上周五， 12 ⽉⻩⾦期货收盘报 3898.90 美元 / 盎司，单⽇跌幅 3.3% ，现货⾦价触及 2025 年 10 ⽉以
来最低⽔平，较 1 ⽉份接近 5600 美元的历史⾼点累计回落约 30% 。⾼盛⼤宗商品团队在其每周报
告中指出，此次跌破 4000 美元发⽣在市场⾯临周末地缘⻛险、整体⻛险偏好收缩的背景下，当

⽇ CME 成交量较前⼀⽇激增 80% ， GLD ETF 单⽇成交约 40 亿美元，位列当⽇全市场 ETF 成交额
第七位。
⾼盛分析师 Robert Quinn 在告中指出，⾃ 9 ⽉ 22 ⽇以来，市场已累计抛售⻩⾦期货头⼨ 195 亿美
元，且抛售节奏持续加快。值得注意的是，此次抛售的主⼒并⾮通常被归咎的对冲基⾦，⽽是
来⾃ " 其他 " 类别及不可报告类别的资⾦，两者合计占总抛售量的约 95% 。与此同时，期权市场的
看跌情绪已升⾄⼀年来的 85 百分位⾼位，市场正在为进⼀步下跌积极布局保护。
这场抛售的核⼼⽭盾在于：⽀撑⾦价的结构性买盘 —— 各国央⾏ —— 依然存在且持续⼊场，但
其买⼊节奏远慢于当前的抛售速度。" 价格不敏感 " 的买家碰上了同样 " 价格不敏感 " 的卖家：央⾏
不在乎价格⾼低照单全收，⽽主权国家等⼤型持仓⽅为满⾜流动性需求，同样不计价格地抛出
⻩⾦这⼀最具流动性的储备资产。两股⼒量的博弈中，速度更快的⼀⽅暂时占据上⻛。
抛售规模与节奏：连续五周、 195 亿美元

据⾼盛对 CFTC 持仓报告的分析，在 10 ⽉ 20 ⽇⾄ 27 ⽇的观察窗⼝内，投机性⻩⾦抛售进⼀步提
速。管理型资⾦、其他类别及不可报告类别合计抛售 80 亿美元，为连续第五周录得看跌资⾦流
出，五周累计抛售规模达 195 亿美元，其中平仓 52 亿美元，新建空头 28 亿美元。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

从规模来看，以约 4000 美元 / 盎司计算， 195 亿美元对应约 490 万盎司，折合约 150 吨⻩⾦。⾼盛
⾃⾝的央⾏购⾦追踪模型显⽰，官⽅部⻔当前购⾦节奏约为每⽉ 85 吨。换⾔之，投机性资⾦在
五周内的抛售量，相当于央⾏近两个⽉的购⾦总量。
更值得关注的是抛售主体的构成。对冲基⾦（管理型资⾦）在此轮抛售中⼏乎缺席，" 其他 " 类别
及不可报告类别合计贡献了约 95% 的抛售量，其中 " 其他 " 类别的多头平仓规模达 40 亿美元，是
最主要的抛售来源。CFTC 定义下的 " 其他 " 类别涵盖家族办公室、⾃营交易商及其他⾮对冲基⾦
性质的⼤型账⼾，属于换⼿率较低、持仓更具粘性的 " 慢钱 " 。
宏观信号全部失灵：主权流动性管理的阴影
此轮⾦价下跌的异常之处在于，所有传统利多因素均已到位。在观察窗⼝内，美国 10 年期实际
利率下⾏ 11 个基点，股市承压，中东地缘局势持续紧张 —— 这三项历来是⻩⾦最可靠的上涨催
化剂。然⽽⾦价在此期间仍下跌 1.6% 。
⾼盛的 Robert Quinn 将部分原因归结为 " 对主权流动性管理的担忧 " 以及美元⾛强。布伦特原油同

期上涨 4.1% ，美元指数上涨 0.6% 。⾼盛指出， " 其他 " 类别多头头⼨与美元及布伦特原油的滚动 6
个⽉相关性均呈 " 适度负相关 "—— 即油价和美元⾛强时，这类多头倾向于平仓离场。
" 主权流动性管理 " 这⼀表述指向⼀个令市场不安的可能性：随着布伦特原油持续上涨、地区冲突
延续，部分⽯油进⼝国乃⾄⽯油出⼝国主权实体可能⽐持有⻩⾦更需要美元，⽽⻩⾦恰恰是其
储备资产中流动性最强的品种。⾼盛同时注意到，近期有⼤量⻩⾦实物流向伦敦 —— ⽽流⼊伦
敦的⾦条，正是可以在伦敦市场出售的⾦条。
头⼨尚未出清：下⾏⻛险仍在
⾼盛明确表⽰，此轮抛售尚未结束。截⾄ 10 ⽉ 27 ⽇， " 其他 " 类别的多头头⼨在名义规模和占未
平仓合约⽐例两个维度上，均仍⾼于两年中位数⽔平。该类别多头头⼨占未平仓合约⽐例在 10
⽉中旬⼀度攀升⾄约 20% ，为⾼盛⼀年期图表中的最⾼值，尽管经历近期抛售后已回落⾄约
18% ，但仍远⾼于 2 ⽉份约 11%-12% 的低点。

### Page 3

COT 数据的截⽌⽇期为 10 ⽉ 27 ⽇，此后市场进⼀步恶化。⾼盛数据显⽰， 10 ⽉ 27 ⽇⾄ 30 ⽇，⾦
价再度下跌 4.3% ，布伦特原油进⼀步上涨 3.7% 成为主要压制因素，美元和实际利率的回落已⽆
法为⾦价提供⽀撑。
在 ETF 层⾯，截⾄上周四， GLD 的持仓量仍距历史⾼点仅约 1% 。⾼盛测算，即便上周五的 ETF
资⾦流出全部来⾃减仓，也不过约 100 万盎司，⽽ GLD 总持仓超过 1 亿盎司。这意味着潜在的抛
售压⼒仍相当可观。
期权市场：看跌情绪升⾄年内⾼位
期权市场的信号同样不容乐观。据 Quinn 报告，⻩⾦ 3 个⽉隐含波动率和经标准化处理的 25 delta
看跌 / 看涨偏斜度均出现上升，后者已达到⼀年期排名的 85 百分位。
⾼盛数据显⽰，偏斜度从 9 ⽉份的约 -10 ⼤幅攀升⾄约 +6 ， 3 个⽉隐含波动率从 2026 年低点附近
的 19% 回升⾄ 21% 以上。上周五单⽇隐含波动率跳升 3.5 个波动率点，两周⾼频实现波动率已运
⾏⾄ 18.5 。六周前的 9 ⽉份，市场争相买⼊看涨期权；如今，看跌期权的溢价已升⾄ 85 百分位⾼

位。
对此，⾼盛⼤宗商品交易台表⽰，⽬前 " 期权和 delta ⻛险敞⼝有限 " ，正在等待重新⼊场的信号，
尚未从多头⽅向部署更多⻛险。
下⼀个⽀撑位与结构性多头的⻓期逻辑
⾼盛⼤宗商品交易台将 3850 美元视为下⼀个关键⽀撑位，但同时承认这是⼀个 " 移动⽬标 " ，并
坦⾔此前预期 3950 美元能够守住的判断已被证伪。 3850 美元距当前期货价格 3898.90 美元不⾜
1.5% ，同时也低于⾼盛商品研究团队上⽉在 " 鹰派美联储下⾏情景 " 中设定的 4070 美元近期底部
—— 彼时该团队认为持续的央⾏购⾦将逐步抬⾼这⼀底部。
⽀撑⾦价的结构性逻辑并未改变：各国央⾏仍在持续购⾦，去美元化趋势未有逆转，⾼盛研究
团队维持⻩⾦ 2027 年底⽬标价 5400 美元不变，并认为官⽅购⾦将是实现这⼀⽬标的主要驱动
⼒。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

数、期权偏斜度处于⾼位、交易台按兵不动、美联储 12 ⽉加息概率被市场定价在约 75% 的背景

台光電 2383 TT ( 電子–電子零組件 )

光耀群倫，電馭群雄 
投資與評價 
1. 台光電 3Q26 受惠在 GPU 新平台 M9 規格產品進入量產，營運將續創新
高；4Q26 各產品線有望調漲 5~20%不等，帶動台光電營運將續創新高。 
2. 26、27 年 GPU 及更多 ASIC 客戶都開始採用 M8+甚至 M9 CCL，高階板
材升級持續 ， 台光電也因應客戶需求逐年開出更多產能 ， 預期營運將連續創
高，預估 26 年 EPS 134.91 元、27 年 EPS 282.30 元。 
3. 考量台光電將持續受惠高階 AI 應用帶動 CCL 規格持續升級，無論技術、產
能擴張幅度皆優於全球同業，26、27 年獲利將大幅走升，目標價上修至 7,00
元(25 x 27(F)PER)，並將投資評等由買進轉為強力買進。 
重點更新 
3Q26 台光電 M9 產品進入量產階段，營運創歷史新高 
台光電 3Q26 受惠於 GPU 客戶下一世代運算平台之 M9 產品正式量產 ， 應用於
UBB(HGX)、LPU 等高速傳輸板材中，大幅提升公司產品平均單價；ASIC 客戶
端需求持續加大，並且將板材全面升級至 M8+規格，高階 CCL 佔比持續擴大，
帶動台光電 3Q26 營收季增 28.8%達 608.85 億元，優於原估之 556.19 億元
(+17.6% QoQ)9.5% 。毛利率方面，公司持續將現有產能往高階產品推動，
M7/M8 等級以上 CCL 出貨佔比快速拉高，毛利率達 37.7%(+3.8 ppts QoQ)，
優於原先預期。且因營收規模擴大，再加上銷售費用控管得宜，營業費用率為
7.0%，優於原預估 7.3%，帶動營業利益達 187.03 億元(+46.7% QoQ)，稅後
淨利季增 46.9%至 145.0 億元，EPS 40.47 元，獲利維持高增長且營運創新高。 
反映原物料持續緊缺之價格調漲，預期台光電 4Q26 營運將續創新高 
邁入 4Q26，預期台光電將持續受惠於各大 AI 客戶於新世代板材規格提升所帶
動的量價齊揚 ， 儘管年底台灣廠區擴廠進度將有所延誤 ， 惟中國崑山廠區亦將有
望提前上線彌補缺口，公司整體產能仍將維持穩定成長。此外，考量上游原材料
供給緊缺依舊，4Q26 台光電 CCL 各產品線有望再度進行 5~20%之價格調漲。
故上修台光電 4Q26 營收預估 18.0%至 721.20 億元(+18.5% QoQ)，並受益於
漲價的持續以及產品組合持續轉佳之下，毛利率預期將再提升 2.6 個百分點至
40.3%；考量產能擴充持續提升下，調高營業費用預估 16.4%至 51.93 億元
(+21.8% QoQ)，惟營業淨利仍將季增 27.8%至 238.76 億元，預估稅後淨利達
186.31 億元(+28.5% QoQ)，EPS 51.99 元，營運將持續創下歷史新高。

### Page 2

訪談內容 
3Q26 台光電 M9 產品進入量產階段，營運創歷史新高 
台光電 3Q26 受惠於 GPU 客戶下一世代運算平台之 M9 產品正式量產， 應用於UBB(HGX)、LPU
等高速傳輸板材中，大幅提升公司產品平均單價；ASIC 客戶端需求持續加大，並且將板材全面
升級至 M8+規格 ， 高階CCL 佔比持續擴大，帶動台光電 3Q26 營收季增 28.8%達 608.85 億元，
優於原估之 556.19 億元(+17.6% QoQ)9.5%。 
毛利率方面，公司持續將現有產能往高階產品推動，M7/M8 等級以上 CCL 出貨佔比快速拉高，
毛利率達 37.7%(+3.8 ppts QoQ)，優於原先預期。且因營收規模擴大，再加上銷售費用控管得
宜，營業費用率為 7.0%，優於原預估 7.3%，帶動營業利益達 187.03 億元(+46.7% QoQ)，稅
後淨利季增 46.9%至 145.0 億元，EPS 40.47 元，獲利維持高速增長且營運達歷史新高。

反映原物料持續緊缺之價格調漲，預期台光電 4Q26 營運將續創新高 
邁入 4Q26，預期台光電將持續受惠於各大 AI 客戶於新世代板材規格提升所帶動的量價齊揚，
儘管年底台灣廠區擴廠進度將有所延誤，惟中國崑山廠區亦將有望提前上線彌補缺口，公司整體
產能仍將維持穩定成長。此外，考量上游原材料供給緊缺依舊，4Q26 台光電 CCL 各產品線有望
再度進行 5~20%之價格調漲。故上修台光電 4Q26 營收預估 18.0%至 721.20 億元(+18.5% 
QoQ)，並受益於漲價的持續以及產品組合持續轉佳之下，毛利率預期將再提升 2.6 個百分點至
40.3%；考量產能擴充持續提升下，調高營業費用預估 16.4%至 51.93 億元(+21.8% QoQ)，惟
營業淨利仍將季增 27.8%至 238.76 億元，預估稅後淨利達 186.31 億元(+28.5% QoQ)，EPS 
51.99 元，營運將持續創下歷史新高。

台光電將開始出貨 M9 CCL 產品，26 年營運創新高 
AI GPU、ASIC 伺服器新平台逐步放量 ， 台光電在技術領先優勢下為其中多家大廠的主要供應商 ，
其中除 25 年主要 ASIC 客戶外，今年另一家美系 ASIC 客戶需求倍數成長，其 CCL 規格要求也
由 M7 提升至 M8+，台光電將在日系同業產能不足情況下取得更高份額。此外 GPU 大客戶首度
採用 M9 規格之產品，將大幅提升單位產品價格，多數客戶訂單預期將持續強勁至年底，加上
1.6T Switch 預期也將於 4Q26 開始進入生產，研究部維持預期 26 年高階 CCL 營收佔比將達
75%不變。 
此外， 考量 4Q26 仍將進行價格調漲政策下 ，故上修整體營收預估 8.3%至 2,133.47 億元
(+126.3% YoY)。考量漲價效益以及產品組合持續優化，大幅調高毛利率預估 6.6 個百分點至
36.4%，並且受益於營運規模擴大與費用控管，調降營業費用率預估至 7.2%(-2.4 ppts YoY)，營
業利益為 624.24 億元(+226.7% YoY)，帶動稅後淨利預期將達 483.44 億元(+230.0% YoY)，優
於原估 408.57 億元之 18.3%，EPS 134.91 元，營運創新高。

CCL 高階升級趨勢不變且將開出更多產能，預期台光電 27 年營運再度創高 
展望 27 年，AI 相關需求在更高傳輸速度及訊號低損耗條件下，終端 PCB 普遍向 M8 以上規格
靠攏，同時板層數也持續往 30 甚至 40 層以上研發，帶動整體 CCL 價量齊揚。而繼GPU 客戶率
先採用 M9 CCL 產品，台光電兩大 ASIC 客戶亦預期將於 27 年導入 M9 規格於，以提升 AI 效能
之極致；衛星方面，台光電目前為地面板供應商之一，同時將進入下世代 v3 版本的天上衛星板
供應，供應 M8 等級 CCL。產能方面，儘管台灣廠區因土建、安規驗收等問題有所遞延，惟預期
截至 4Q27 仍將有 870 萬張月產能，相較 4Q26 將年增近五成，預期將滿足客戶未來新世代高
階 CCL 產品備貨需求，並使營運進一步擴大，維持產業高市佔之表現。

### Page 4

預期台光電在 M8+ CCL 以上領域仍為客戶供應商首選，加上產能進一步擴大開出下，上修 27
年整體營收預估 13.9%至 3,768.70 億元(+76.6% YoY)，伴隨 M9 出貨量持續放大，同時預期更
多終端應用將 M8+ CCL 作為主要板材選擇，加上產能緊缺與原料問題有望持續進行價格調整，
大幅上修毛利率預估 4.8 個百分點至 41.6%，且受益於營收規模持續大幅提升，調低營業費用率
至 6.9%(-0.3 ppts YoY)，預期營業利益為 1,296.54 億元(+107.7% YoY)，帶動稅後淨利年增
109.2%至 1,011.55 億元，EPS 282.30 元，獲利將翻倍成長且營運再度創高

技術、產能均穩居業界龍頭，台光電 26、27 年獲利將持續大幅走升 
台光電 26 年於 AI、高階交換器，甚至衛星客戶需求強勁成長，以及漲價策略帶動下營運將季季
增，27 年持續維持領先技術為客戶 M9 CCL 首選，且大量新產能開出也將成為未來幾年台光電
在高階 CCL 市場中保持高市佔的關鍵，中長期營運持續成長，預期至27 年獲利仍保持年增超過
八成，預估 26 年 EPS 134.91 元、27 年 EPS 282.30 元。 
目前台光電股價為 5,025 元，約為 17.08 x FY27E EPS，位於近 3 年 PER 參考區間 10~30x 中間
偏下之位置。儘管目前於最高規格之 CCL 解方出現分歧，Q-Glass 石英布與 PTFE 等無布方案皆
受到高度討論，惟研究部認為製程良率、成本管控上將會是材料選用、量產之關鍵，且材料定案
時程仍尚需要一些時間，惟整體 CCL 產業材料持續升級趨勢不變下，在規格和用量上皆持續向
上，持續看好台光電於高階產品應用、客戶廣度，以及在現有、擴增產能規模上持續領先同業，
將有助公司持續維持高市佔之表現，26、27 年獲利將持續大幅走升，調高 27 年獲利預估，並將
投資評等由買進轉為強力買進，目標價由 5,300 元(25x 修正前 27(F)PER)上修至 7,000 元(25x 修
正後 27(F)PER)。

### Page 5

公司簡介 
台光電子材料股份有限公司成立於 1992 年 3 月，為全球最大無鹵素 CCL 供應商，公司產品
主要包含：銅箔基板、黏合片與多 層壓合板等，以終端需求進行區分，3Q26 年手持式營收占比
10~15%，基礎建設營收占比 75~ 80%，車用和其他占 5~10%。

台光電 ESG 政策 
環境：台光電子秉持「環境保護與永續經營並重」的理念，將環境管理納入核心經營策略，並積
極推動  能源管理及碳排放管理。公司已於  2024 年成功將每單位銷售金額的能源消耗量較  
2023 年減少 23.01%， 並將溫室氣體排放總量降至 23,080.51 公噸 ， 減少了 29.70%的排放強
度。此外，台光 電子也已完成對大陸各廠區 ISO 50001 能源管理系統的驗證，並推動太陽能發
電項目 ， 進一步 提升綠能使用比例 。 原材料方面 ， 所有主要銅箔供應商均可提供 100%回收銅 ，
體現資源循環利 用的承諾。展望未來，台光電子以 2030 年減碳 30%為目標，並計劃於 2050 
年達成淨零排放， 持續致力於推動環境永續。  
社會： 台光電子認為員工是公司最重要的資產，致力於員工的多元共融與專業成長。公司在  
2024 年達 成了 85%員工年齡在 50 歲以下，且大力推動女性在管理層的參與，女性主管比例
達 10%以上。 此外，公司秉持員工健康與安全的優先理念，所有員工的健檢率達 100%，並提
供完善的職業安  全衛生管理。台光電子也積極參與社區建設，並在台灣廠區認養並維護超過  
300 平方米的綠地， 為員工與社區提供更綠意盎然的環境。 
公司治理： 台光電子始終秉持誠信、透明與問責的公司治理理念，並將永續發展納入董事會的
決策範疇 。 公 司治理架構中 ， 董事會設有專責的企業永續發展委員會 ， 負責審議並推動各項 ESG 
議題。台光電 子已成功納入「台灣永續指數」 ，並以透明的財務報告與風險管理策略，確保公司
治理與股東利 益一致。此外，公司也對供應鏈進行管理，確保其符合企業社會責任準則，並且
確保所有主要供 應商達到環保與社會責任的要求。  
永續供應鏈與長期展望： 台光電子在推動永續供應鏈管理上，已要求供應商遵守  RBA 行為準
則與衝突礦產規範，並且積 極實施供應商的環境與社會責任評估。隨著 5G 基站材料與車用電
子需求的增長，台光電子將持 續推動高附加價值產品，並以低碳製造與責任治理為長期發展重
心。公司已設定以 2023 年為基 準年，並計劃在 2030 年減碳 30%，在 2050 年達成淨零碳
排，致力於成為全球電子材料領域的 永續競爭者。

## Document 470: 【國泰證期研究部】網通產業-調變未定通道先行400G電路將到來-20261029

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】網通產業-調變未定通道先行400G電路將到來-20261029.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1 
產業更新-網通產業 
調變未定通道先行，400G SerDes 將到來 
What’s New – 規格已定，訊號編碼方式未決 
資料中心交換器的埠速每隔幾年翻倍，方法是把每條線路的速度加倍，下世代的3.2T 需 8 條線路、
每條 400G，也就是 400G SerDes，即光互連論壇(Optical Internetworking Forum, OIF) 的 CEI-
448G。 該規格的框架文件已於2025 年 11 月發布 ，2026 年 2 月並啟動極短距與長距兩項子規格設
定，但這代首次遇到既有線路無法負荷，沿用現行PAM4 編碼需要線路承受 112GHz 頻率，但受連
接器限制只有約 90GHz，雖然沒有明言 PAM4 無法運作；但改用 PAM6 編碼可降到約 87GHz，代
價是訊號品質下降，且光訊號那端仍是 PAM4 須多一層轉換。兩者差距不只在頻率，階數越多則每
階間距越窄，糾錯負擔與晶片複雜度同步上升，關鍵在連接器能否再往上突破一成以上的頻寬，而
不在編碼技術本身是否有新解。 
Trends/Outlook – 三種線路方案同時出現 
速度越快 ， 銅線能傳的距離越短，標準規格的 112G 世代還有 2 公尺 、224G 世代降到 1 公尺 ，400G
世代連目標都未訂，除非以主動銅纜或繞過電路板的纜線設計可再延長。距離縮短迫使光學元件往
晶片旁移動，規格要求把晶片與到鄰近光引擎 的距離定在約 150mm，這正是近封裝光學 (Near-
Packaged Optics, NPO)的定義，意即 400G 電路就是 NPO 出現的原因，產業因而分三種方案。一
是拉高線路速度並換連接器以延續可插拔，NVIDIA 與思科的 USFP 對上 Arista 與 MSA 的 XPO，
前者單模組容量 12.8Tbps、頻寬與前面板密度為現行 4 倍，後者的多源協議成員半年內由 25 家增
至逾 150 家；二是把光學搬到晶片旁或封裝內的 NPO 與共同封裝光學(CPO)；三是 Credo 的寬而
慢，以數百條慢速光通道取代少數高速通道。 
Investment Summary 
1、 400G SerDes 的導入時程將呈現晶片先到、周邊生態系後到 的狀態，預期 博通支援 400G 
SerDes 的交換器晶片 TH7 將有機會於 1H27 問世，28 年則有配合 TH7 的第 4 代 CPO 模組
推出，乙太網聯盟的次世代晶片同樣列於 28 年，主機端的 PCIe Gen8 規格亦預計 28 年才完
成。224G/1.6T 或 224G/800G 仍是 26 與 27 年各廠商的主要貢獻獲利來源；未來 448G 的
瓶頸不全是因為晶片本身，尚有連接器、基板材料與主機端介面等週邊生態系需完備。 
2、 對台廠而言，將來為了導入 400G SerDes 架構，無論選擇 USFP 或 XPO，交換器前面板、連
接器與液冷模組都須重新設計，與晶片商的整合能力更為重要，同時可滿足 CSP 客戶在電訊
號與光通訊的需求，具 Tomahawk 平台開發能力的智邦 (2345 TT) 兩者皆可承接；貿聯 -
KY(3665 TT)既發表電訊號的 448G 互連方案，又是 Credo 寬而慢生態系整合商，為唯一同時
站上兩條路線的台廠。目前瓶頸落在被動零件與材料，受惠順序應由連接器、線材與高階基板
材料端向週邊網卡與交換器擴散。

訊號編碼與線路條件的取捨 
單一通道傳輸速度要提高，通常往兩種方向發展，一是每秒送出更多次訊號，二是每一次訊號多
帶幾個位元 。 早期乙太網到每通道25G 至28G 為止採用不歸零編碼(Non-Return-to-Zero, NRZ)，
訊號只有高低兩種狀態、一次帶一個位元；從 56G 世代起改用四階脈衝振幅調變 (Pulse 
Amplitude Modulation 4-level, PAM4)，把訊號分成四個高度、一次帶兩個位元，送出次數因而
減半，這正是過去三個世代能沿用既有線路的原因。到了 400G 世代，PAM4 與 PAM6 的差異如
表一與圖一所示，階數越多，每秒送出的次數與線路要承受的頻率越低，但每一階之間的間距也
越窄、越容易判讀錯誤。PAM6 還多一個麻煩，六不是二的整數倍，必須用兩次訊號傳五個位元
的方式折衷，編解碼複雜度明顯高於 PAM4，這也是它遲遲無法定案的原因之一。 
表一、NRZ、PAM4 與 PAM6 三種訊號編碼方式比較 
比較項目 NRZ (PAM2) PAM4 PAM6 
電壓準位數 2 4 6 
每符號位元數 1 2 log₂6≈2.585(實作 2.5) 
448Gbps 下符號率 448 GBd 224 GBd 173.31 GBd 
448Gbps 下奈奎斯特頻率 224 GHz 112 GHz 86.66 GHz 
相對 PAM4 之 SNR 差(BER 1e-5) 約+9.5dB(理論) 0dB(基準) -3.62dB 
現行通道(約 90GHz)可行性 不可行 不可行 可行 
與光域 IMDD PAM4 對齊 否 是 否(須 6 對 4 gearbox) 
支援 LPO/線性驅動 否 是 否 
向後相容 56G/112G/224G 否 是 否 
FEC 額外負擔 低 5.8%(KP4) 約 12%+1.5~2%(MPI) 
主要應用世代 ≤28G/通道 56G~224G/通道 448G 候選 
資料來源：OIF-FD-CEI-448G-01.0、OIF 448G AI Workshop，國泰證期研究部整理 
圖一、NRZ / PAM4 / PAM6 Signalling at 448 Gbps

資料來源：OIF-FD-CEI-448G-01.0，國泰證期研究部 
編碼方式最後是由線路條件決定，OIF 指出目前 448G 線路的頻寬上限只有約 90GHz，主要卡在
連接器；晶片封裝端反而不是問題，封裝接腳間距1.0mm 對應 58GHz、0.65mm 對應 90GHz、
0.5mm 對應 115GHz，而0.5mm 以下已可量產 ， 意即封裝本身已能支撐PAM4 所需的 112GHz，
真正的瓶頸在連接器與各段轉接。訊號衰減的要求也同步收緊，56G 世代允許 30dB、224G 世代
為 40dB，400G 世代則要在更高的頻率下仍維持 40dB 以上，連帶使高階基板材料與連接器成為
能否成案的關鍵，而非晶片設計本身。表二也列出各級介面的耗電目標，從最短距離的 0.5 pJ/b
到最長的 2.5 pJ/b，距離越遠越耗電，這也說明縮短電的傳輸距離本身就是省電手段，並且是後
面評估寬而慢路線時應有的比較基準。

表二、A Projection of OIF CEI-448G IAs and Characteristics 
比較項目 XSR(極短距) VSR(超短距) MR(中距) LR(長距) Linear(線性驅動) 
資料速率(Gbps) 288–462 288–462 288–462 288–462 288–462 
糾錯後/前位元錯誤率 1E-15/<1e-6 1E-15/5e-5 1E-15/5e-6 1E-15/1e-4 1E-15/2e-4 
最長距離 50 mm 220 mm 500 mm 1000 mm 120 mm 
互連組成 電路板，無連接器 電路板+1 連接器 電路板+1 連接器 電路板+2 連接器 電路板+1 連接器 
插入損耗(dB) 約 15 約 30/35 約 30/35 約 35/40 約 22 
訊號編碼方式 PAM4 PAM8/6/4? PAM8/6/4? PAM8/6/4? PAM4 
系統耗電目標(pJ/b) 0.5 0.8 1.8 2.5 <2.5 
是否需糾錯 需要 需要 需要 需要 需要 
資料來源：OIF、Ethernet Alliance TEF 2025，國泰證期研究部整理 
訊號品質不足的部分可以靠糾錯編碼 (Forward Error Correction, FEC) 補回來，但能補的幅度有
限。OIF 明言現行的糾錯能力已不足以支撐 448G，必須再加一層額外編碼。華為在 OIF 研討會
提出的比較顯示，把糾錯能力從 6.9dB 提升到 7.8dB，代價是額外負擔由 5.8%升至 12.1%、運
算複雜度增加近五倍；而 PAM6 本身就需要約 12%的額外負擔，等於在起跑點就先付出一筆成
本。換句話說，PAM6 在頻率上省下來的，有相當一部分會在糾錯這項功能還回去。 
從晶片實作來看，PAM4 要做到 400G，類比電路的頻寬必須加倍、雜訊與抖動要改善一倍，製
程得推進到 2 奈米以下；PAM6 雖因送出次數較少而在部分電路上較省，但等化與判讀電路的複
雜度分別增加約五成與一倍 ， 綜合下來只略優於PAM4， 省下的電力大致剛好用在額外的糾錯上 。
結論是電與光最好維持同一種編碼，以免每種架構都要多一層轉換；PAM6 的技術風險較低，但
PAM4 在光的那一端與抗干擾上明顯較好，最終要由模組、線性驅動與銅纜三類設計共同決定。
值得注意的是，OIF 已明確表示業界對折衷速率並無需求，意即 400G 電路沒有中間站，產業必
須一次跨過這道門檻，沒有緩衝的速度可選，採用時程因此格外嚴謹。 
USFP、XPO 與寬而慢 
既然上限來自連接器，400G 電路的架構分流實際上由連接器進度決定。Amphenol 在 OCP 峰會
把 224G 之後的瓶頸依序排為晶片封裝出線第一、可插拔介面第二；TE Connectivity 則表示現行
OSFP 規格沒有通往 448G 的明確路徑。這也解釋了標準目標與實際系統的落差：規格中的 1 公
尺，是電路板走線、兩個連接器與纜線合計落在約 40dB 損耗預算內的結果；若把纜線直接接到
封裝、繞過電路板出線，同樣的預算就能讓給纜線本身。NVIDIA 的 NVL72 機櫃即屬此類，背板
以逾 5,000 條銅纜完成櫃內互連，總長超過 2 英里，而單條長度仍在 1 公尺以內，並未脫離同一
量級 。 四家主要供應商提出兩類互不相容的解法 ：Amphenol 與鴻騰精密(Foxconn Interconnect 
Technology, FIT)保留現行的卡緣(Card edge)接法，靠收緊公差與縮小接點間距克服；TE 與立訊
(Luxshare)則改用兩件式接頭，以金屬外框把每一對訊號完全包覆。四種設計的差別，最後體現
在頻寬能撐到多高，也就是能否達到 PAM4 所需的 112GHz，這道門檻決定了整體架構的走向，
也決定了哪些供應商進得了這一代的名單，時間壓力因此落在被動零件端。 
表三、OCP 2026 各廠 448G 可插拔連接器方案比較 
方案/廠商 介面型式 頻寬 超出 PAM4(112GHz)餘裕 差動阻抗 資料性質 
224G 可插拔(Amphenol 範例) 卡緣 約 78 GHz 不足 約 85Ω 已量產出貨 
XPO 448G(Amphenol) 卡緣、公差收緊 約 123 GHz 約 10% 89~92Ω 推估曲線、展場 demo 
XPO 448G(鴻騰精密 FIT) 卡緣、新接點 約 118 GHz 約 5% 89~92Ω 模擬，原型待實測 
MegaLane(TE Connectivity) 兩件式、無殘段 約 126 GHz 約 13% 約 92Ω 模擬，TE 稱已有實測 
448G OSFP(立訊 Luxshare) 兩件式 約 111 GHz 幾近於零 92Ω 與 85Ω 併行 模擬，已有樣品 
資料來源：OCP Global Summit 2026 各廠簡報，國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 4

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/10/29

4 
如表三所示，四個 448G 設計都能跨過 PAM6 所需的約 90GHz，但相對 PAM4 所需的 112GHz
餘裕只有 0 至 14GHz。換言之，PAM4 能否成立幾乎完全押在連接器能否再往上推一成以上，這
也是 OIF 遲遲不敢定案的原因；Amphenol 甚至為了達到 120 至 130GHz，XPO 可能必須犧牲
與舊規格的相容性。400G SerDes 的編碼，本質上不是訊號處理技術之爭，而是連接器這類被動
零件的機構與公差之爭，勝負取決於幾家連接器廠的製造精度。 
NVIDIA 與思科 2026 年 10 月 12 日共同發表的超高密度小型可插拔(Ultra-Dense Small Form 
Factor Pluggable, USFP)，是PAM4 路線的第一個商業化產品。 這是首款支援每條線路400Gbps
的模組，在與現行 OSFP 相同的外形內放進 16 條雙向通道、單模組容量達 12.8Tbps，未來可再
擴充到 32 條以上；相較目前每條 200Gbps 的模組，頻寬與前面板密度提升為四倍、能源效率約
1.3 倍，並可在不另外配置散熱迴路的條件下支援最高 100 瓦；相較之下，現行 1.6T OSFP 世代
單模組功耗約 40 瓦，卻已須外掛液冷籠座與冷板組件散熱，如圖四，USFP 等於把散熱責任收回
模組本身。兩家公司明言其光學設計是以簡化訊號處理來降低延遲與耗電，方向指向線性驅動可
插拔光模組(Linear Pluggable Optics, LPO) ，而 LPO 唯一可行的編碼正是 PAM4。生態系已與
TE Connectivity、Amphenol 合作機殼與連接器，並計畫以開放規格推出。值得注意的是 TE 表
示 USFP 採用其 MegaLane 接頭，正是四種方案中頻寬餘裕最高的兩件式設計，顯示NVIDIA 與
思科選擇以更換接頭型式來換取 PAM4 的可行性，而非在編碼上退讓，等於把這一代是否能導入
的成敗因素交給連接器供應商的製造精度。 
圖二、XPO (eXtra-dense Pluggable Optics) Connector, Cage and Plug Module 
資料來源：鴻騰精密(FIT)，國泰證期研究部整理 
圖三、USFP 12.8Tb/s per module        圖四、Current 1.6T OSFP liquid-cooled 2X8 cage 
資料來源：NVIDIA、立訊，國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 5

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/10/29

5 
與 USFP(Ultra-Dense Small Form Factor Pluggable) 正面競爭的是 Arista 主導、鴻騰精密與
Amphenol 同屬卡緣陣營的超高密度可插拔光學(eXtra-Dense Pluggable Optics, XPO)。該協議
2026 年 3 月成立時約 25 家成員，至 OCP 峰會已逾 150 家；Arista 揭露的模組規格為單模組
12.8Tbps、整合冷板可散熱至每模組 400 瓦，與 USFP 同屬 12.8Tbps 等級；Amphenol 揭露的
連接器規格則涵蓋 64 條高速通道、單一48V 供電、整合液冷並相容MPO-16 光介面。兩套方案
的根本差異在於，XPO 保留現行卡緣接法以沿用既有機構與散熱設計，改版責任落在模組端的小
電路板上；USFP 則接受更換接頭型式，以換取更高的頻寬餘裕。對台廠而言，無論最後收斂到哪
一方，前面板、機殼、連接器與液冷模組都必須重新設計，交換器的工程價值反而因此提高，具
Tomahawk 平台開發能力的智邦(2345 TT) 在兩套規格下都能承接設計與製造，不必在規格定案
前押注任何單一陣營。 
第三條路線乾脆不參加速度競賽，Credo 在 OCP 峰會以 Wider. Slower. Better.為題主張，光學
應該在可靠度、耗電與成本這三項原本屬於銅纜的強項上取勝，作法是用數百條慢速的微型發光
元件取代少數高速通道，把加速壓力留在電的那一端。其耗電論據是：銅纜加上晶片電路約需
5pJ/bit，微型發光元件則低於 2pJ/bit，其中紅光微型 LED 低於 1pJ/bit、可達 2 公尺，藍光低
於 2pJ/bit、可達 10 公尺，微型雷射低於 2pJ/bit、可達 50 公尺。不過這個比較的基準是銅纜方
案；若改與表二中 OIF 對線性介面訂的 2.5 pJ/b 目標相比，優勢會明顯收斂。以 NVIDIA VR200 
NVL72 機櫃估算，銅纜方案約耗 10kW、約佔整櫃用電 5%，改用微型發光元件約可降到 4kW、
省下約六成 ， 相當於每顆GPU 省 80 瓦 。 產品方面 ，Credo 已於 10 月 12 日發表 1.6T 主動光纜 ，
以約 200 條光纖承載、單端耗電約 12 瓦、可達 50 公尺，刻意採用最簡單的 NRZ 編碼而不使用
訊號處理晶片 ， 並內建備援通道 ， 預計2027 年量產 ； 其路線圖為2027 年 1.6T 光纜與 6.4T NPO、
2028 至 2029 年 3.2T 光纜與 CPO。生態系中發光元件由 ams OSRAM 供應，光纖與連接器涵
蓋康寧(Corning)等廠，整合商則包含 Credo、貿聯(BizLink)與正崴(Foxlink)，三家整合商中台廠
即占兩席，是寬而慢路線上最直接的台廠受惠位置，惟兩家目前皆屬組裝與整合角色，尚未切入
上游的發光與光學元件，單位價值相對有限。 
圖五、µEmitter-Based Optical Interconnect Roadmap 
資料來源：Credo，國泰證期研究部整理 
須強調三條路線並非互斥，而是對應不同的傳輸距離。規格定義的距離由短到長依序為：封裝內
晶片對晶片約 25mm、晶片到封裝內光引擎約 50mm、晶片到鄰近光引擎約 150mm、晶片到可
插拔模組約 250mm、同一塊電路板內約 50cm、跨板或跨機箱約 1 公尺。其中 150mm 這一級
正是 NPO 的定義，而120mm 的線性級別明確指定使用 PAM4。也就是說，400G 電路在壓縮銅
線可達距離的同時，等於直接在規格裡為 NPO 與線性驅動留下位置，預期 NPO 也將於 27 年底
至 28 年進入市場，貿聯-KY(3665 TT)則同時出現在 448G 高速互連與寬而慢這兩條路線上。

### Page 6

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/10/29

6 
主機端同步倍速，PCIe 世代為何決定網卡速度 
400G 電路的時程之所以能相對確定地落在 2028 年之後 ， 還有一個主機端的原因 。 網卡的工作是
把線路上收到的每一個位元搬進主機記憶體 ， 反之亦然 ， 因此主機介面的頻寬不能低於網路埠速 。
主機介面就是 PCIe，它的世代因而與乙太網埠速形成一對一對應，背後邏輯與前面的線路完全相
同：每一代把每條線路的速度加倍，也在同一代遇上編碼方式的轉折。以業界標準的 x16 插槽計
算，PCIe Gen5 每條線路 32GT/s、單向有效頻寬約 505Gbps，承載 400G 埠速還有 1.26 倍餘
裕；但面對 800G 只剩 0.63 倍、短缺約 37%。這不是設計餘裕不夠，而是結構性不足，這正是
Gen5 只能支撐到 400G 的原因，與設計優劣無關。 
表四、PCIe 世代與可承載乙太網埠速對應關係 
PCIe 世代 每通道速率 調變與編碼 x16 單向有效頻寬 可承載埠速 頻寬餘裕 代表網卡 規格發布 
Gen4 16 GT/s NRZ、128b/130b 約 252 Gbps 200G 約 1.26 倍 — 2017 年 
Gen5 32 GT/s NRZ、128b/130b 約 505 Gbps 400G 約 1.26 倍 ConnectX-7 2019 年 
Gen6 64 GT/s PAM4、1b/1b+FLIT+FEC 約 968 Gbps 800G 約 1.21 倍 Thor Ultra 2022 年 
Gen7 128 GT/s PAM4、1b/1b+FLIT+FEC 約 1,936 Gbps 1.6T 約 1.21 倍 — 2025 年 6 月 
Gen8 256 GT/s 待定 約 3,872 Gbps(雙向 1TB/s) 3.2T 約 1.21 倍 — 
2025 年宣布
預計 2028 年 
資料來源：PCI-SIG、Broadcom、AMD、Marvell，國泰證期研究部整理 
PCIe Gen6 把每條線路速度倍增到 64GT/s，並在同一代完成兩項與乙太網完全相同的轉折：編
碼由 NRZ 改為 PAM4， 並改用新的封包格式搭配糾錯 ，x16 單向有效頻寬因而提升到約968Gbps，
承載 800G 有 1.21 倍餘裕。這個對應關係可由實際產品驗證：博通 2025 年 10 月發表的業界首
款 800G AI 網卡 Thor Ultra，明確採用 PCIe Gen6 x16 主機介面；AMD 在 Helios 機櫃所列的
第二代 Pensando Vulcano 網卡同樣是 800G、 同樣走Gen6， 每顆GPU 配置三張 、 合計2.4Tbps。
兩款 800G 網卡都無法在 Gen5 上成立，也說明 800G 網卡必須與 Gen6 同時問世，兩者的時程
實為同一條，網卡無法在主機介面到位前先行推出。 
依同樣的倍增邏輯，PCIe Gen7 已於 2025 年 6 月發布規格，每條線路 128GT/s、x16 單向約
1,936Gbps，對應 1.6T 埠速同樣是 1.21 倍餘裕；Gen8 則規劃每條線路 256GT/s、x16 單向約
3,872Gbps、雙向約 1TB/s，對應的正是 3.2T 埠速，也就是400G 電路世代。Marvell 已於 2026
年 2 月在 DesignCon 展示運行於 256GT/s 的 PCIe 8.0 電路——在規格尚未完成、系統尚未成
形的階段就先展示晶片，反映的正是主機介面與網路介面必須同代到位的結構性約束。值得注意
的是，PCIe 的線路要穿過插槽、轉接卡與較長的板上走線，條件比網路側更嚴苛，因此它的編碼
方式同樣還沒定案，面臨與 400G 電路相同性質的取捨。 
把三個層面合在一起看，400G SerDes 電路能否成立不取決於單一元件的突破，而是編碼方式、
連接器頻寬與主機介面三者必須同時到位：編碼的取捨懸在連接器能否再推進一成以上的頻寬，
連接器的兩種路線已分別由 Arista 主導的 XPO 與 NVIDIA、思科的 USFP 代表，主機端則要等
PCIe Gen8 規格於 2028 年完成 。 而三者的時程並不同步 ， 呈現的是晶片先到 、 周邊生態系後到。
依通路訪查，預期博通支援 400G SerDes 的交換器晶片 TH7 將有機會於 1H27 問世，28 年則有
配合 TH7 的第 4 代 CPO 模組推出，乙太網聯盟的次世代晶片同樣列於 28 年，主機端的 PCIe 
Gen8 規格亦預計 28 年才完成。224G/1.6T 或 224G/800G 仍是 26 與 27 年各廠商的主要貢獻
獲利來源；未來 448G 的瓶頸不全是因為晶片本身，尚有連接器、基板材料與主機端介面等週邊
生態系需完備。受惠順序因而由連接器、線材與高階基板材料端向週邊網卡與交換器擴散，對應
台廠為貿聯-KY(3665 TT)與智邦(2345 TT)。

## Document 510: サイバー攻撃による経済損失をどう考えるか ～一企業の被害から、マクロ的な経済損失となるリスク～

Source PDF: all report pdf/サイバー攻撃による経済損失をどう考えるか ～一企業の被害から、マクロ的な経済損失となるリスク～.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年10 月 14 日

1/2 
サイバー攻撃による経済損失をどう考えるか 
～一企業の被害から、マクロ的な経済損失となるリスク～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  前田 和馬 
（℡：050-5473-3888）

情報漏洩を中心にサイバー攻撃による被害が国内外で目立っている。相次ぐサイバー攻撃には
AIが悪用された可能性が指摘されており、今後もこうした被害は拡大する懸念がある。サイバー
攻撃による経済的な影響は主に3 つ考えられる。 
 まず、被害による直接的な金銭損失であり、仮想通貨等の資産の流出、身代金の支払い、被害
者への補償金支払いなどが挙げられる。FBIが被害申告ベースでまとめた「Annual Internet 
Crime Report 2025」によると、米国を中心としたサイバー犯罪の被害総額（個人被害も含む）は
209 億ドルと近年飛躍的に拡大している。なお、こうした被害は基本的に所得移転であり、生産活
動を捕捉する国内総生産（GDP）に直接的な影響を与えるわけではない。 
 次に、サイバー攻撃を通じた企業活動への損害だ。例えばCSISとMcAfeeによる 2020 年の調査で
は、サイバー攻撃を受けた場合に業務は平均18 時間中断したと指摘する。サイバー攻撃は既存顧
客へのサービス提供や新たな顧客獲得活動などを抑制する懸念がある。これに加えて、企業のブ
ランド価値を毀損し、顧客は該当サービスの利用を当面ためらうかもしれない。 
 サイバー攻撃による経済活動への被害が一企業に留まるのであれば、競合他社が代替的にサー
ビスを提供する、或いはシステム復旧後に挽回生産などが生じることで、マクロ的な影響は限定
される。しかし、こうした被害が「交通インフラなど代替が難しい企業」、或いは「産業内の広範
な企業」に及ぶ場合、サイバー攻撃で損なわれた供給能力を補うことが難しく、マクロ経済全体
の損失へと波及する。AIを通じたサイバー攻撃の頻発は、従来のミクロ的な「一企業の被害」か
ら、マクロ的な「産業や経済全体の被害」に転じるリスクを高めている可能性がある。 
 最後に、サイバーセキュリティ対策の強化によるIT関連支出の増加であり、これはGDPを押し上
げる可能性が高い。例えば、2000 年問題（西暦が2000 年になるとコンピュータが誤作動を起こす
との懸念）の際のIT対策費用は全世界で3,000～6,000 億ドルと、1999 年における世界GDPの 0.9
～1.8%に達したとみられている。一方、攻撃をうける企業の防御対策よりも、AI脅威論による開
発抑制の動きが強まる場合、データセンター投資や株式市場の調整を通じて景気減速へと繋がる
懸念がある。なお、米国ではトランプ政権が対中競争力の観点からAI規制に消極的であるほか、
連邦議会におけるFrontier ActなどのAI規制法案も成立に至るかは不透明だ。

### Page 2

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。

景気は弱い動き
中国景気は弱い動きが続いている。9月の製造業PMIは50.1と前月から上昇し、好不況の境目である50を3カ月ぶりに上
回った。構成指数をみると、生産は大きく上昇した一方、就業人員の低下が全体の改善ペースを抑制した。非製造業PMI
は50.2と3カ月ぶりに50を上回ったが、過去と比べると低水準で推移している。なお、2026年4～6月期の実質GDP成長
率は前年比＋4.3%と伸びが鈍化し、季節調整済み前期比（年率換算）も＋3.6%と政府目標の「4.5～5%」を下回った。

為替は元高、株価は下落
人民元の対ドルレート（9月末）は1ドル＝6.71元だった。大幅な貿易黒字に加え、米国政治・経済の先行き不透明感を背
景としたドルへの慎重な見方が根強く、ドル安・元高が進んだ。8月末の外貨準備高は3兆4,383億ドルと増加した。
9月の上海総合株価指数は下落した。月中旬まで横ばいで推移したが、月末の米中首脳会談の成果が市場の期待を下
回ったことから株価は下落し、約2カ月ぶりの安値を記録した。業種別指数は、材料やエネルギーの下落が大きかった。

9月の米中首脳会談は一定の成果も、中心は対話継続の確認
9月24日、米国・ワシントンで米中首脳会談が実施された。5月の会談で打ち出された「建設的戦略安定関係」の構築を再
確認したほか、年内あと2回の会談の可能性が示唆されるなど、首脳間の対話の継続が確認された（下図）。
具体的には、米中双方が300億ドル相当の品目で関税引き下げに合意したほか、AI分野で対話枠組みを構築する方針で
合意するなど、個別分野では一定の進展が得られた。もっとも、総じてみると内容は概ね想定内であり、米中対立の全面
的な解消には至っていない。貿易戦争の休戦も今年11月までとされた期限が2カ月延長されたにとどまっており、今後も
両者間で対話を継続することを確認したことが最大の成果だったといえよう。

輸出価格は上昇も、デフレ輸出への懸念はなお強い
中国の輸出価格が足元で上昇している（左図）。2023年以降、EVや太陽光パネル、鉄鋼を中心に輸出価格は下落傾向
にあり、廉価品の輸出が海外市場を席巻する「デフレ輸出」が国際問題となったが、2026年4月以降、状況が一変した。品
目別でみると、世界的に需給が逼迫している集積回路やPC、PC部品の価格上昇が顕著である。
もっとも、足元の価格上昇は一部の品目に偏っている。HSコード4桁で分類した全1,204品目のうち、足元でも5割近くで価
格が下落している（右図）。このため、デフレ輸出への懸念は依然として根強い。

## Document 555: 何处是底

Source PDF: all report pdf/何处是底.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

尽管价值投资者应该在 “ 他⼈恐惧时贪婪 ” ，但判断何时是真正的 “ 恐惧 ” 是最⼤的难度。国⾦
策略认为，对⽐ 2007-2008 年周期股和市场交易指标，按照历史极端⾏情看，当下并不具
备抄底条件。如果交易指标进⼀步出现极端值且配合美股的企稳，可能才是阶段性反弹的
时机。
摘要：
摘要
1  拐点的出现：海外科技巨头的投资回报再度受到市场的审视
从本周美股云⼚商财报来看， AI 相关投⼊持续⾼企，但盈利与现⾦流的兑现速度仍难以匹配资本
开⽀的扩张幅度，未来投资回报与融资可持续性并未获得明确保证。当前云业务收⼊的持续增
⻓意味着 AI 投⼊正在转化为可量化的投资回报，符合预期；但与此同时，新世界与旧世界的⽭盾
也在激化，⾕歌⼴告与亚⻢逊电商收⼊增速均边际下⾏，在新业务的发展尚不⾜以带来充裕现
⾦流的同时，传统业务增⻓动能的⾛弱则削弱了当下云⼚商依靠内部现⾦流⽀撑巨额资本开⽀
的能⼒。⽽从债务与收⼊的视⻆来看，随着云⼚商外部融资规模的扩张，利息⽀出也开始对利
润产⽣⼀定的侵蚀，典型的如亚⻢逊利息⽀出占净利润⽐重由 25 年平均 3% 提升⾄ 26Q3 的

8.2% 。尽管新业务确实在增⻓，但传统业务受损叠加利息负担加重，反⽽使得⾕歌和亚⻢逊⾃
由现⾦流出现⼤幅回落，这也意味着当下云⼚商仍难以形成 “ 云业务增⻓ → 现⾦流修复 → 资本开
⽀扩张 ” 的正向循环。财报披露后各家云⼚商不同的业绩表现也使得股价表现存在差异，微软因
能够实现正⾃由现⾦流⽽获得市场的 “ 奖励 ” ，财报披露后 EPS 上调且股价上涨；⽽亚⻢逊、⾕歌
则因业绩 miss （财报披露后 EPS 下调）、现⾦流骤降⽽出现股价⼤跌。⽬前看，对美债利率⽽
⾔，随着⼀致预期中 2026Q4 的 AI 资本开⽀放缓，美债利率的顶部可能已经出现，但考虑到当下
美国经济基本⾯仍具备韧性，未来美债利率的下⾏幅度则仍有待观测，利率的快速下⾏仍需要
等待 AI 融资出现快速收缩的下⼀个路标信号。
2  科技调整的幅度观测： 2007 周期股 VS2026 的硬件科技
从两个维度来看科技调整的幅度：（ 1 ）此前我们中期策略提出本轮 AI 产业周期可类⽐于 2005-
2007 年中国的制造业投资周期。对⽐ 2007-2008 年周期股与本轮科技股股价的回撤有相似之

处：本轮科技股⾃ 7 ⽉初调整以来回撤幅度约 54% ，出现在美国科技股只是上⾏逆转但未出现明
显调整之前； 2007-2008 年美股⻅顶并未出现次贷危机前，中国周期股于 2007 年 10 ⽉⻅顶⾄
2008 年 6 ⽉回撤幅度为 62% 。但值得关注的是， 2008 年美股真正的加速下跌是在 2008 年 8 ⽉之
后，彼时则对应次贷危机⻛险升级、产业链量价⻬跌；与此同时， A 股也未能幸免， 2008 年 8 ⽉
之后全 A 仍有 40% 以上的跌幅，相关周期板块跌幅也接近 50% 。当下来看，中国 AI 硬件相关资产
是美国上游供应商，和中国在 2007 年加⼊ WTO 后是美国需求供给⽅有类似，后续需要密切关注
美国科技与债务周期情况。⽬前来看，尽管云⼚商模式的可持续性仍有担忧，但 AI 商业化路径确
实正在体现，仍在分歧时期。（ 2 ）从市场交易指标来看：①两融担保⽐例本周继续回落；②主
要宽基指数隐含波动率上⾏，当前科创 50 、创业板指、中证 1000 的隐含波动率均已升⾄ 3 年 90%

以上的分位数⽔平，但上述两个指标距离历史极值仍有⼀定距离。③市场宽度来看， A 股整体与
科技股内部均已降⾄历史低位。当前交易结构确实已经开始具备底部条件，但交易指标距离历
史极值还有⼀定距离则意味着倘若尾部⻛险出现，相关指标向历史极值靠拢仍有可能给市场带
来下⾏⻛险。值得⼀提的是，当前 AI 硬件超配⽐例仍⾼于⼤多历史主流赛道超配⽐例的峰值，主
动偏股基⾦对 AI 硬件整体的持仓暴露依然较⾼。结论是，按照历史极端⾏情看，当下并不具备抄
底条件。如果交易指标进⼀步出现极端值且配合美股的企稳，可能才是阶段性反弹的时机。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

3  盈利下⾏时期：应对市场波动的结构性线索
当前企业盈利的周期性放缓开始体现在经济数据和企业财报当中：今年 6 ⽉以来⼯业企业利润增
速连续 4 个⽉边际回落，与此同时上市公司 2026Q3 利润端开始出现降温迹象。结构上，以盈利
正增⻓占⽐所衡量的盈利扩散度在 2021 年以来仅⾼于 22Q4 ，景⽓依然稀缺，利润增⻓主要集中
于少数⾼景⽓⾏业。值得关注的是，尽管⾮科技领域整体盈利有所放缓，但部分⾏业的景⽓韧
性仍在，与其在科技板块的⾼波动中反复博弈，不如将视野拓展⾄⾮科技领域，财报视⻆下可
关注⾼景⽓延续组合（共 12 个⾏业如煤炭开采）和困境反转组合（共 7 个⾏业如炼化及贸易）。
4  为未来的⾏情积蓄⼒量
尽管价值投资者应该在 “ 他⼈恐惧时贪婪 ” ，但判断何时是真正的 “ 恐惧 ” 是最⼤的难度。作为这轮
参与全球产业链映射的中国投资者⽽⾔，国内市场的下跌幅度本⾝并不是衡量 “ 恐惧 ” 最好的尺
度，这也是抄底过程中最⼤的陷阱。在美国科技巨头资本开⽀拐点出现后，我们对未来 1-2 年资

本成本的回落、科技应⽤的开花与中国资产的崛起充满信⼼，现在我们将致⼒于等到这天的出
现。我们推荐：第⼀，以煤炭、⽯油、油运为代表的能源链将继续受益于全球对能源的补库需
求，能源链是资本成本波动与地缘冲突下的韧性资产；第⼆，财报视⻆下建议关注：①⾼景⽓
延续：医疗服务、专业连锁、煤炭开采、航运港⼝，以及有⾊、化⼯中的细分领域等；②困境
反转策略：纺织服装的细分领域，以及造纸、炼化及贸易、医疗器械等。第三，⻩⾦在实际利
率拐头时期可能会受到流动性冲击，但参考 2008 年经验⼤的机会也在因此孕育。
⻛险提⽰：国内经济修复不及预期；海外经济⼤幅下⾏。
报告正⽂
1  拐点初现：海外科技巨头的投资回报再度受到市场的审视
随着科技⾏情⾏⾄下半场，当前市场对 AI 的关注也正在转向投资回报的验证阶段，然⽽从本周美
股云⼚商财报来看，⽬前并未给市场提交⼀份令⼈满意的答卷： AI 相关投⼊持续⾼企，但盈利与
现⾦流的兑现速度仍难以匹配资本开⽀的扩张幅度， “ 造⾎ ” 能⼒尚未跟上 “ 烧钱 ” 的速度，未来投

资回报和融资可持续并未获得明确的保证。从云⼚商本⾝的业务收⼊来看， 2026Q3 亚⻢逊
AWS 、⾕歌 Cloud 和微软 Azure 收⼊分别实现 39.3% 、 102.6% 和 48.2% 的同⽐增速，需求仍在⾼
速增⻓， AI 投⼊正在转化为可量化的投资回报，也符合预期；但与此同时，新世界与旧世界的⽭
盾也在激化，云⼚商传统业务的受损正在体现在财务报表当中，其中⾕歌的⼴告业务、亚⻢逊
的在线商店收⼊增速边际下⾏（ 2026Q3 同⽐分别为 10.6% 和 1.6% ），在新业务的发展尚不⾜以
带来充裕现⾦流的同时（ 2026Q3 微软、⾕歌、亚⻢逊云业务占整体收⼊⽐重分别为 36% 、
24% 、 23% ），传统业务增⻓动能的边际⾛弱在带来业绩 miss 的同时也削弱了云⼚商依靠内部现
⾦流⽀撑巨额资本开⽀的能⼒。

### Page 3

从债务与收⼊的视⻆来看，⾃从去年四季度开始云⼚商转为依赖外部融资之后，当下云⼚商在
⻓期借款出现明显增加的同时（尤其是亚⻢逊和⾕歌），利息⽀出也开始对利润产⽣⼀定的侵
蚀，例如 2025 年亚⻢逊利息⽀出占净利润⽐重平均约为 3% 左右，但 2026 年以来逐季增加，
2026Q2 开始达到 8.2% 。⽽相⽐之下， “ 优等⽣ ” 微软在保持相对债务融资相对克制的同时其利息
⽀出占净利润的⽐重则维持相对稳定的⽔平。尽管新业务确实在增⻓（对应的也是 AI 商业化路径
正在得到验证），但传统业务受损叠加外部融资增加后所带来的利息负担加重，反⽽使得巨额资
本开⽀下 2026Q3 部分云⼚商的⾃由现⾦流出现⼤幅回落，这也意味着当下云⼚商仍尚未形成 “ 云
业务增⻓ → 现⾦流修复 → 资本开⽀扩张 ” 的正向循环。值得⼀提的是，财报披露后各家云⼚商不
同的业绩表现也使得股价表现存在差异，微软因能够实现正⾃由现⾦流⽽获得市场的 “ 奖励 ” ，财
报披露后 EPS 上调且股价上涨；⽽亚⻢逊、⾕歌则因业绩 miss （财报披露后 EPS 下调）、现⾦流

骤降⽽出现股价⼤跌。
对美债利率⽽⾔，过去 AI 投资的持续扩张⽀撑了美债利率的上⾏，然⽽当下云⼚商的盈利模式尚
未形成闭环，尽管资本开⽀仍在增加，但未来增速放缓的拐点可能已经出现。随着市场对前期 AI
产业乐观预期的修正，美债利率的顶部可能已经出现。但考虑到当前美国经济基本⾯仍具韧
性， 2026Q3 美国 GDP 超预期，投资侧依然对美国经济形成⽀撑，同时 AI 投资向其他领域的扩散
也还在持续，未来美债利率的下⾏幅度则仍有待观测，这是我们前⾯⼏期周报讲到的最⻘⻩不
接的时候。

### Page 4

2   当前 A 股科技股的位置
从海内外的科技股表现来看，尽管美股以费半为代表的 AI 硬件在上半周出现企稳，但随着云⼚商
财报披露，市场再度出现反复波动；与此同时，本周 A 股科技股板块也出现明显回调。实际上，
⾃从今年 6 ⽉底以来， A 股电⼦、通信为代表的科技股回撤幅度已达甚⾄超过 50% 。⽽站在当前
时点来看，在股价经历显著回调、 AI 产业的可持续性仍处于验证期的背景下，⽬前 A 股科技股已
经回落⾄什么位置，以及前期调整是否已释放相关⻛险则正在成为市场关注的核⼼问题。
2.1 类⽐ 2007-2008 年周期股的下跌
此前我们在中期策略报告中曾指出本轮 AI 产业周期来看，真实的盈利、随处可⻅的短缺与通胀、
以及投资预期占全球 GDP ⽐例抬升的程度，与当年中国加⼊ WTO 后的中国制造业投资中的周期
⽜市存在诸多相似之处。⽽参考 2005-2007 年⾏情周期⻅顶的原因，并⾮是单纯的资⾦⾯破灭所
致，⽽是⾦融条件、产业链利润分配格局以及外部宏观环境多⽅因素的共振。对⽐ 2007-2008 年

周期股与本轮科技股股价的回撤有相似之处：本轮科技股⾃ 7 ⽉初调整以来回撤幅度约 54% ，出
现在美国科技股只是上⾏逆转但未出现明显调整之前； 2007-2008 年美股⻅顶并未出现次贷危机
前，中国周期股于 2007 年 10 ⽉⻅顶⾄ 2008 年 6 ⽉回撤幅度为 62% 。⽽从股价和估值的调整节奏
来看，本轮科技股的下跌斜率明显更为陡峭，市场对产业预期的修正与估值收缩的速度也更
快。

### Page 5

值得关注的是，从 2008 年美股的表现来看，美股真正的加速下跌实际上是在 2008 年下半年。
2007Q2 之后全球 GDP 已经陷⼊增⻓瓶颈，⽽⼤宗商品价格仍在上涨，产业链陷⼊ “ 价升量减 ” 的
不稳定状态，类似于经济周期中的 “ 滞胀 ” 状态，市场开始定价盈利下修的⻛险。⽽随着 2008 年 6
⽉雷曼提前透露第⼆财季可能出现亏损，尤其是 8 ⽉房利美、房地美相继披露⼆季度亏损，市场
对⾦融体系⻛险的担忧逐步升温；尤其是在 9 ⽉雷曼破产之后流动性冲击与资产抛售则使得美股
开始出现快速下跌。与此同时， A 股也未能幸免，全球⾦融危机爆发、外需⾛弱的冲击下， 2008
年 8 ⽉⾄ 11 ⽉万得全 A 仍有 40% 以上的跌幅，周期股跌幅则接近 50% 。当下来看，中国 AI 硬件相
关资产是美国上游供应商，和中国在 2007 年加⼊ WTO 后是美国需求供给⽅有类似，后续需要密
切关注美国科技与债务周期情况。⽬前来看，尽管云⼚商尚未形成盈利的正向循环， AI 产业链未
来投资回报与融资可持续性仍有待验证，但云业务的增⻓也说明 AI 商业化路径正在逐步验证，仍

在分歧时期。
2.2 交易指标已符合底部区域，但距离历史极值仍有距离
上周周报中我们提出观察市场位置的两个交易指标：融资融券担保⽐例和宽基指数期权隐含波
动率。⽽从最新情况来看：①融资融券担保⽐例截⾄周四数据为 250% ，倘若考虑周五的调整后
（以万得全 A 周五跌幅 2.34% 测算）可能进⼀步回落⾄ 244% 左右。⽬前 3 年分位数、回撤、去杠
杆均满⾜我们上周周报所提及的底部条件，交易结构已得到⼀定的出清。不过，倘若与历史极

### Page 6

端⽔平进⾏对⽐，如 24 年 1 ⽉的 220% 、 25 年 4 ⽉的 240% ，当前两融的担保⽐例距离历史极值仍
有⼀定的距离。②本周主要宽基指数的隐含波动率均出现上⾏，尤其是科创 50 、创业板指、中
证 1000 的隐含波动率均已上升⾄近三年 90% 以上的分位数⽔平，交易情绪逐步接近底部区域。
不过，与两融担保⽐例相类似，当前隐含波动率距离历史极值也还有⼀定差距。③此外，从市
场宽度来看，⽆论是 A 股整体还是科技板块内部，当前市场宽度均已降⾄历史低位，尤其是以电
⼦、通信为代表的科技板块，市场宽度已创下年内新低。因此，当前交易结构确实已经开始具
备底部条件，但交易指标距离历史极值还有⼀定的距离则意味着倘若尾部⻛险出现，相关指标
仍可能向历史极值靠拢，市场也不排除存在下⾏⻛险的可能。
值得⼀提的是，从筹码结构来看，⽬前依然出清较为缓慢， 2026Q3 主动偏股基⾦对于 AI 硬件 ( 光
模块、 PCB 、半导体等 ) 的配置⽐例 / 超配⽐例快速回落⾄ 45.2%/26.4% ，但⽆论是与 AI 硬件⾃⾝

⽐、还是对⽐历史其他主流赛道资产，主动偏股基⾦对于 AI 硬件的配置 / 超配⽐例依然处于历史
⾼位，值得⼀提的是，当前 AI 硬件的超配⽐例仍⾼于⼤多历史主流赛道超配⽐例的峰值。即虽然
三季度 AI 硬件遭遇较⼤的回撤，但主动偏股基⾦对于 AI 硬件的减持幅度较为有限，整体的持仓暴
露依然较⾼。

### Page 7

3   盈利下⾏时期：应对市场波动的结构性线索
当前企业盈利的周期性放缓开始体现在经济数据和企业财报当中：⾃今年 6 ⽉以来⼯业企业利润
总额累计增速已连续 4 个⽉下滑，与此同时上市公司盈利在经历 2025 年以来的持续修复之后，
2026Q3 利润端开始出现降温迹象，全 A （⾮⾦融地产）的归⺟净利润整体 / 扣⾮⼝径下分别相较
Q2 边际回落 4.3/1.9 个百分点⾄ 11.2%/11.3% 。结构上，以盈利正增⻓占⽐所衡量的盈利扩散度
在 2021 年以来仅⾼于 22Q4 ，景⽓依然稀缺，利润增⻓仍主要集中于少数⾼景⽓⾏业，尚未形成
⼴泛的盈利扩散。
值得关注的是，尽管相⽐科技的⾼增⻓，⾮科技领域整体盈利有所放缓，但随着海外 AI 投资回报
的可持续再度受到审视，与其在科技板块的⾼波动中反复博弈，不如将视野拓展⾄⾮科技领
域，部分⾏业的景⽓韧性可能使得相关板块具备性价⽐，或将是配置结构再平衡中值得关注的
重要线索。⽽从财报视⻆下，我们主要从两条思路进⾏筛选：
（ 1 ）⾼景⽓延续：从业绩增速绝对值视⻆下， 2026Q3 营业收⼊与归⺟净利润单季度增速排名

均处于前 30 的⼆级⾏业，包括：有⾊⾦属（⼩⾦属、能源⾦属、⼯业⾦属）、医药⽣物（医疗服
务）、商贸零售（专业连锁）、煤炭（煤炭开采）、交通运输（航运港⼝）、基础化⼯（化学原
料、化学纤维、⾮⾦属材料）、机械设备（⾃动化设备）等。

### Page 8

（ 2 ）困境反转：边际视⻆来看，我们设定连续两个季度营收增速、归⺟净利润增速与 ROE
（ TTM ）边际改善作为景⽓度持续性回升的判断依据（如果已⼊选⾼景⽓延续组合则从困境反
转组合中剔除），最终筛选出来的⾏业包括：有⾊⾦属（⾦属新材料）、轻⼯制造（造纸）、⽯油
⽯化（炼化及贸易）、建筑装饰（装修装饰）、纺织服装（纺织制造、纺织家纺）、医药⽣物（医
疗器械）等。
4   为未来的⾏情积蓄⼒量
尽管价值投资者应该在 “ 他⼈恐惧时贪婪 ” ，但判断何时是真正的 “ 恐惧 ” 是最⼤的难度。作为这轮
参与全球产业链映射的中国投资者⽽⾔，国内市场的下跌幅度本⾝并不是衡量 “ 恐惧 ” 最好的尺
度，这也是抄底过程中最⼤的陷阱。在美国科技巨头资本开⽀拐点出现后，我们对未来 1-2 年资
本成本的回落、科技应⽤的开花与中国资产的崛起充满信⼼，现在我们将致⼒于等到这天的出
现。基于以上内容，我们推荐：
第⼀，以煤炭、⽯油、油运为代表的能源链将继续受益于全球对能源的补库需求，同时也是资
本成本⾼位震荡 + 地缘冲突下的稳定资产；

第⼆，财报视⻆下建议关注：①⾼景⽓延续：医疗服务、专业连锁、煤炭开采、航运港⼝，以
及有⾊、化⼯中的细分领域等；②困境反转策略：纺织服装的细分领域，以及造纸、炼化及贸
易、医疗器械等。
第三，对于⻩⾦⽽⾔，实际利率下⾏前半段可能会受到流动性冲击，可以等待时机。
⻛险提⽰

### Page 9

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

海外经济⼤幅下⾏：如果海外经济超预期下⾏，那么全球制造业共振修复可能会暂停，实物资

▎Fed 按兵不動，年底升息預期仍存 
10 月底 FOMC 如預期維持利率於 3.75%至 4%不變，
決議以 10 比 2 通過，2 名官員主張升息 1 碼。會後
Fed 重申維持物價穩定仍是政策重點，主席華許也肯
定經濟與就業表現，但未對 12 月利率行動提供明確指
引。不過，美國第 3 季 GDP 年化成長 3.4%，優於預
期，9 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 年增率亦升至 3.6%與
3.1%，顯示經濟動能仍強、通膨壓力仍在，支撐市場
預期年底再升息 1 碼。

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10/29 
▎避險需求升溫，殖利率續降支撐債市 
近期 AI 與科技股賣壓升高，市場避險需求增溫，帶動
資金流入美債。雖然中東情勢反覆、油價仍處高檔，
通膨疑慮尚未消退，但大型科技公司財報再度引發市
場對 AI 投資與估值的疑慮，推升避險買盤，10 年期
美債殖利率降至 7 週低位。隨殖利率回落，10 月以來
主要債種自低點反彈，其中公債與投資級債回升較
快，今年以來銀行投等債則展現相對較佳抗跌性。 
圖 2 殖利率回落支撐債市修復，今年銀行債相對抗跌

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10/30 
◼ Fed 如預期按兵不動，第 3 季 GDP 成長 3.4%、優於預期，PCE 通膨續高，反映
經濟與物價仍具韌性，年底升息預期仍存。股市震盪推升避險需求，10 年期殖利
率大幅回降至 4.9%附近，債券自低檔持續反彈，今年銀行投等債表現相對抗跌。 
◼ 美國 6 大銀行 3Q 財報整體穩健，核心利息收益仍具韌性，NII 合計升至約 800
億美元，貸款與存款亦維持成長。全球大型銀行不良資產比率降至 0.17%，資產
品質維持良好 ， 第一類資本與流動性水準仍具充足緩衝 ， 整體信用體質保持穩健 。 
◼ 從產業配置來看，大型銀行信用體質穩健，且各家仍具不同獲利支柱。摩根大通*
受惠多元業務 ， 美國銀行*與富國銀行*核心銀行業務穩健 ， 花旗*、 高盛*與摩根士
丹利*則由服務、交易及財富管理支撐。加上目前美債殖利率仍處相對高檔，銀行
投等債具備較佳收益條件，配置吸引力進一步提升。

▎美國大型銀行財報穩健，獲利韌性佳 
美國大型銀行第 3 季財報整體維持穩健，6 大銀行中，
富國銀行*、花旗*、高盛*及摩根士丹利*獲利優於市場
預期，摩根大通*與美國銀*行則受交易、投行業務較弱
及部分一次性項目影響，獲利低於預期。 第3 季長天期
美債殖利率走高、市場波動加大，使部分固定收益交易
與資本市場業務較上半年降溫 ， 但大型銀行核心業務仍
展現韌性 。從銀行核心獲利來看 ，美國 6 大銀行第 3 季
淨利息收入（NII）合計升至約 800 億美元，延續近年
上升趨勢，顯示核心利息收益仍具韌性。

款分別較去年同期成長 6.8%及 5.7%， 顯示放款需求與
存款基礎仍維持穩健成長。
